			or value.kind!=INSTALLED_DUGOUT_D1_KIND or not Codec.whole(value.version,23,23) \
			or not Codec.keys(value.current,["active","ordinal","carried","archive"]) \
			or value.current.active!="dynasty" or not Codec.whole(value.current.ordinal,4,4) \
			or not value.current.archive is Array or value.current.archive.size()!=1: return null
	var link: Variant=value.current.archive[0]
	if not Codec.keys(link,["ordinal","format","sha256","bytes"]) \
			or not Codec.whole(link.ordinal,3,3) or not Codec.whole(link.format,22,22) \
			or not Codec.whole(link.bytes,1,MAX_BYTES) or link.bytes!=payload.size() \
			or link.sha256!=BaseArchive._hash(payload): return null
	var replay:=_earned_dynasty_fourth_stage(payload,source,first,second)
	return replay.season if not replay.is_empty() and Codec.same(replay.staged,value) else null


# Native v23 reader shares the Season 3 journal, with an ordinal-bound
# predecessor link. A close is allowed only on a paid terminal image.
static func _earned_dynasty_later_head(value: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not Codec.keys(value,["kind","version","head","current"]) \
			or value.kind!=INSTALLED_DUGOUT_D1_KIND or not Codec.whole(value.version,23,23) \
			or not value.head is Dictionary or BaseArchive._head(BaseArchive._wire(value.head)).is_empty() \
			or not Codec.keys(value.current,["active","ordinal","archive","carried"]) \
			or value.current.active!="dynasty" or not Codec.whole(value.current.ordinal,4,ClubCareer.MAX_RUNS): return {}
	var ordinal: int=int(value.current.ordinal)
	var pages: Variant=value.current.archive
	if not pages is Array or pages.size()!=1 or not Codec.keys(pages[0],["ordinal","format","sha256","bytes"]) \
			or not Codec.whole(pages[0].ordinal,ordinal-1,ordinal-1) \
			or not Codec.whole(pages[0].format,22 if ordinal==4 else 23,22 if ordinal==4 else 23) \
			or not BaseArchive._digest(pages[0].sha256) or not Codec.whole(pages[0].bytes,1,MAX_BYTES): return {}
	var wire: Variant=value.current.carried
	if not wire is Dictionary: return {}
	var fields: Array=["event","season"]
	for key: String in ["opening","shop","regular","playoffs","close"]:
		if wire.has(key): fields.append(key)
	if wire.has("workshop_chair"):
		if not wire.has("close") or not _earned_dynasty_workshop_chair_shape(wire.workshop_chair,ordinal): return {}
		fields.append("workshop_chair")
	if wire.has("workshop_layout"):
		if not wire.has("close") or not _earned_dynasty_workshop_layout_shape(wire.workshop_layout): return {}
		fields.append("workshop_layout")
	if wire.has("workshop_applied"):
		if not wire.has("close") or not _earned_dynasty_workshop_application_shape(wire.workshop_applied,ordinal): return {}
		fields.append("workshop_applied")
	if not Codec.keys(wire,fields) or not wire.season is Dictionary \
			or not Codec.keys(wire.event,["id","from","to","seed","binding","before","after","destination"]) \
			or not wire.event.id is String or not Codec.whole(wire.event.from,ordinal-1,ordinal-1) \
			or not Codec.whole(wire.event.to,ordinal,ordinal) or not Codec.whole(wire.event.seed,0,2147483647) \
			or not BaseArchive._digest(wire.event.before) or not BaseArchive._digest(wire.event.after) \
			or not BaseArchive._digest(wire.event.destination) or not wire.event.binding is Dictionary \
			or wire.event.binding.get("kind")!="dynasty-common-carry-v2": return {}
	if wire.has("regular") and (not wire.has("opening") or not wire.opening is Dictionary or wire.opening.is_empty()): return {}
	if wire.has("playoffs") and not wire.has("regular"): return {}
	if wire.has("opening") and not _earned_dynasty_second_round_wire(wire): return {}
	if wire.has("shop") and (not wire.has("opening") or wire.opening.is_empty() \
			or not wire.shop is Array or wire.shop.is_empty() or wire.shop.size()>128): return {}
	if wire.has("close"):
		if not wire.has("regular") or wire.regular.size()!=9 or not wire.season.physical.pending.is_empty() \
				or not Codec.whole(wire.season.state.get("phase"),SeasonState.Phase.COMPLETE,SeasonState.Phase.COMPLETE) \
				or not Codec.keys(wire.close,["common","local","ledger","workshop"]) \
				or not wire.close.common is Dictionary or not wire.close.local is Dictionary \
				or not wire.close.ledger is Dictionary or not wire.close.workshop is Dictionary: return {}
	return value


# The replayed Close funds this one purchase; Season Cash and its baseball
# journals never change. An archived purchase is inherited, not reissued.
static func _earned_dynasty_workshop_chair_shape(record: Variant,ordinal: int) -> bool:
	return Codec.keys(record,["version","lineage","ordinal","source","prefix_sha256","ledger"]) \
		and Codec.whole(record.version,1,1) and Codec.whole(record.ordinal,ordinal,ordinal) \
		and record.lineage is String and BaseArchive._digest(record.source) \
		and BaseArchive._digest(record.prefix_sha256) and record.ledger is Dictionary


static func _earned_dynasty_workshop_layout_shape(record: Variant) -> bool:
	return Codec.keys(record,["version","lineage","layout"]) and Codec.whole(record.version,2,2) \
		and record.lineage is String and record.layout is Dictionary


static func _earned_dynasty_workshop_layout(close: Dictionary,birth_seed: int,season: SeasonState,
		chair: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if chair.is_empty(): return {}
	var carrier:=_earned_dynasty_workshop_owner(close,birth_seed,season)
	if carrier==null: return {}
	carrier.workshop_chair_purchase=chair
	return Owner.WorkshopApply.paid_draft(carrier)


static func _earned_dynasty_workshop_application_shape(record: Variant,ordinal: int) -> bool:
	return Codec.keys(record,["version","lineage","closed_ordinal","source","snapshot"]) \
		and Codec.whole(record.version,2,2) and Codec.whole(record.closed_ordinal,ordinal,ordinal) \
		and record.lineage is String and BaseArchive._digest(record.source) and record.snapshot is Dictionary


static func _earned_dynasty_workshop_application(close: Dictionary,birth_seed: int,season: SeasonState,
		chair: Dictionary,layout: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if chair.is_empty() or layout.is_empty(): return {}
	var carrier:=_earned_dynasty_workshop_owner(close,birth_seed,season)
	if carrier==null: return {}
	carrier.workshop_chair_purchase=chair
	var authored: Dictionary=Owner.WorkshopApply.paid_draft(carrier)
	if authored.is_empty() or not Codec.same(authored,layout): return {}
	carrier.workshop_draft=authored
	return Owner.WorkshopApply.issue(carrier)


static func _earned_dynasty_workshop_owner(close: Dictionary,birth_seed: int,season: SeasonState) -> RefCounted:
	if not _earned_dynasty_close_boundary(season) or close.is_empty(): return null
	var carrier: RefCounted=Owner._new()
	carrier.active="dynasty";carrier.session=season;carrier.status="closed"
	carrier.ordinal=int(close.local.ordinal);carrier.birth_seed=birth_seed
	carrier.last_close=close.local;carrier.ledger=close.ledger;carrier.workshop_close_history=close.workshop
	return carrier


static func _earned_dynasty_workshop_receipt_valid(close: Dictionary,birth_seed: int,season: SeasonState,
		record: Dictionary) -> bool:
	var carrier:=_earned_dynasty_workshop_owner(close,birth_seed,season)
	if carrier==null: return false
	var authored: Dictionary=Owner.WorkshopPurchase.issue(carrier)
	return not authored.is_empty() and Codec.same(authored,record)


static func _earned_dynasty_compact_head(value: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	return _earned_dynasty_later_head(value) if value is Dictionary and value.get("version")==23 \
		else _earned_dynasty_third_head(value)

static func _earned_dynasty_compact_wire(value: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	return value.current.carried if value.version==23 else value.current.third


static func _earned_dynasty_archived_compact(link: Dictionary,base_head: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var path:=_installed_dugout_d1_page_path(link.sha256)
	if path.is_empty() or not _base_archive_absent(path+".tmp"): return {}
	var member:=_base_archive_member(path)
	if member.is_empty() or not member.present or member.bytes.size()!=link.bytes \
			or BaseArchive._hash(member.bytes)!=link.sha256: return {}
	var old:=_earned_dynasty_compact_head(Owner._bytes_value(member.bytes))
	if old.is_empty() or old.version!=link.format or old.current.ordinal!=link.ordinal \
			or not Codec.same(old.head,base_head) or not _earned_dynasty_compact_wire(old).has("close"): return {}
	return old


# Read-only worker independently proves every sealed season before the live
# claim. Its caller already owns the exact ordinary and v20 replay result.
static func _earned_dynasty_replay_predecessors(source: Dictionary,first: SeasonState,second: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	var plan:=_earned_dynasty_third_verified(source,first,second)
	for link: Dictionary in source.dynasty_compact_history:
		var archived:=_earned_dynasty_archived_compact(link,source.head)
		if archived.is_empty(): return {}
		var wire:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(archived)
		var previous:=_earned_dynasty_third_current(plan,wire)
		if previous==null: return {}
		var bought: Dictionary=wire.get("workshop_chair",plan.get("inherited_chair",{}))
		var layout: Dictionary=wire.get("workshop_layout",plan.get("inherited_layout",{}))
		var applied: Dictionary=wire.get("workshop_applied",plan.get("inherited_application",{}))
		plan=_earned_dynasty_carried_plan(first,previous,wire.close,int(archived.current.ordinal)+1,applied)
		if not plan.has("wire"): return {}
		plan.inherited_chair=bought
		plan.inherited_layout=layout
		plan.inherited_application=applied
	return plan


# Called only after current installed revision validation or an exact warm
# binding. The worker already proved the sealed history. Hydrate the parked
# terminal, then reconstruct just the active season's typed runtime.
static func _earned_dynasty_native_active_plan(source: Dictionary,first: SeasonState,second: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	var history: Array=source.dynasty_compact_history
	if history.is_empty(): return _earned_dynasty_third_verified(source,first,second)
	var archived:=_earned_dynasty_archived_compact(history.back(),source.head)
	if archived.is_empty(): return {}
	var wire:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(archived)
	var previous:=Adapter.restore("dynasty",wire.season)
	if not _earned_dynasty_close_boundary(previous) or not Codec.same(Adapter.capture("dynasty",previous),wire.season): return {}
	var applied: Dictionary=source.get("dynasty_inherited_application",{})
	var plan:=_earned_dynasty_carried_plan(first,previous,wire.close,source.dynasty_compact_ordinal,applied)
	if plan.has("wire"):
		plan.inherited_chair=source.get("dynasty_inherited_chair",{})
		plan.inherited_layout=source.get("dynasty_inherited_layout",{})
		plan.inherited_application=applied
	return plan


static func _earned_dynasty_third_prior(payload: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	if payload.is_empty() or payload.size()>MAX_BYTES: return {}
	var value: Variant=Owner._bytes_value(payload)
	if not value is Dictionary or not Codec.whole(value.get("version"),20,20): return {}
	return _installed_dugout_d1_parts(value)

# Verification is detached and read-only. Actual file publication belongs to
# the installed revision-checked transaction, never this framing helper.
static func _earned_dynasty_third_stage(payload: PackedByteArray,first: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	var previous:=_earned_dynasty_third_prior(payload)
	if previous.is_empty(): return {}
	return _earned_dynasty_third_frame(payload,previous,_earned_dynasty_prepare_third(previous,first))

static func _earned_dynasty_third_frame(payload: PackedByteArray,previous: Dictionary,plan: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if not plan.has("wire"): return {}
	var value: Dictionary={"kind":INSTALLED_DUGOUT_D1_KIND,"version":21,"head":previous.head,
		"current":{"active":"dynasty","ordinal":3,
			"archive":[{"ordinal":2,"format":20,"sha256":BaseArchive._hash(payload),"bytes":payload.size()}],
			"third":plan.wire}}
	var staged:=_native_materialize(value)
	if staged.is_empty() or _earned_dynasty_third_head(staged.value).is_empty(): return {}
	return {"encoded":staged.bytes,"staged":staged.value,"season":plan.season}

# Processing a head is never authority on its own. Even its content hash
# binds only exact predecessor bytes. Those bytes still have to replay and
# produce the same transition, with the same durable common awards.
static func _earned_dynasty_third_restore(head: Variant,prior: PackedByteArray,first: SeasonState) -> SeasonState:
	var value:=_earned_dynasty_third_head(head)
	if value.is_empty(): return null
	var page: Dictionary=value.current.archive[0]
	if prior.size()!=page.bytes or BaseArchive._hash(prior)!=page.sha256: return null
	var previous:=_earned_dynasty_third_prior(prior)
	if previous.is_empty() or not Codec.same(value.head,previous.head): return null
	return _earned_dynasty_third_current(_earned_dynasty_prepare_third(previous,first),value.current.third)

static func _earned_dynasty_third_current(plan: Dictionary, wire: Dictionary) -> SeasonState:
	if not plan.has("wire") or not Codec.same(wire.event,plan.wire.event): return null
	if not wire.has("opening"):
		return plan.season if Codec.same(wire.season,plan.wire.season) else null
	if not _earned_dynasty_second_round_wire(wire): return null
	var base: SeasonState=plan.season
	var witness: Dictionary=wire.season.physical.pending if wire.opening.is_empty() else wire.opening
	base.physical.pending=witness.duplicate(true)
	var opening:=_earned_dynasty_opening_plan(base)
	if opening.is_empty(): return null
	var current: SeasonState=base if wire.opening.is_empty() else opening.candidate
	if not wire.opening.is_empty() and not _earned_dynasty_opening_settled(current,wire.opening): return null
	for command: Variant in wire.get("shop",[]):
		if not command is Dictionary: return null
		current=_installed_earned_dynasty_shop_step(current,command)
		if current==null: return null
	if wire.has("regular"):
		var source: Dictionary={"dynasty":wire.season,"dynasty_regular":wire.regular,
			"dynasty_postseason":wire.has("playoffs"),"dynasty_playoffs":wire.get("playoffs",[])}
		current=_earned_dynasty_replay_regular(current,source)
		if current==null: return null
		if source.dynasty_postseason:
			current=_earned_dynasty_replay_playoffs(current,source)
			if current==null: return null
	if not Codec.same(Adapter.capture("dynasty",current),wire.season): return null
	if wire.has("close"):
		var close:=_earned_dynasty_third_close_record(plan.close_prior,plan.birth_seed,current,wire.season)
		if close.is_empty() or not Codec.same(close,wire.close): return null
		if wire.has("workshop_chair") and (not plan.get("inherited_chair",{}).is_empty() \
				or not _earned_dynasty_workshop_receipt_valid(close,plan.birth_seed,current,wire.workshop_chair)): return null
		if wire.has("workshop_layout") or wire.has("workshop_applied"):
			var chair: Dictionary=wire.get("workshop_chair",plan.get("inherited_chair",{}))
			if wire.has("workshop_layout"):
				if not plan.get("inherited_layout",{}).is_empty(): return null
				var layout:=_earned_dynasty_workshop_layout(close,plan.birth_seed,current,chair)
				if layout.is_empty() or not Codec.same(layout,wire.workshop_layout): return null
			if wire.has("workshop_applied"):
				if not plan.get("inherited_application",{}).is_empty(): return null
				var saved_layout: Dictionary=wire.get("workshop_layout",plan.get("inherited_layout",{}))
				var apply:=_earned_dynasty_workshop_application(close,plan.birth_seed,current,chair,saved_layout)
				if apply.is_empty() or not Codec.same(apply,wire.workshop_applied): return null
	elif wire.has("workshop_chair") or wire.has("workshop_layout") or wire.has("workshop_applied"): return null
	return current


# v16 stores one opener witness. The transition's destination still commits
# to the original immutable, zero-game v15 birth before any baseball is played.
static func _earned_dynasty_second_round_wire(next: Dictionary) -> bool:
	if not next.opening is Dictionary or not next.season.get("state") is Dictionary \
			or not next.season.get("physical") is Dictionary: return false
	var physical: Dictionary=next.season.physical
	if not physical.get("pending") is Dictionary: return false
	var pending: Dictionary=physical.pending
	if next.opening.is_empty():
		return not next.has("regular") and Codec.whole(next.season.state.get("round_index"),0,0) \
			and _earned_dynasty_opening_pending(pending)
	if not _earned_dynasty_opening_pending(next.opening) or next.opening.reports.size()!=2: return false
	if next.has("playoffs"):
		return next.get("regular") is Array and next.regular.size()==9 \
			and _earned_dynasty_regular_wire(next.regular,next.season,{},next.playoffs) \
			and _earned_dynasty_playoff_wire(next.playoffs,next.season,pending)
	if next.has("regular"): return _earned_dynasty_regular_wire(next.regular,next.season,pending)
	return Codec.whole(next.season.state.get("round_index"),1,1) and pending.is_empty()


static func _earned_dynasty_verified_second(source: Dictionary,first: SeasonState) -> SeasonState:
	var next: Dictionary=source.dynasty_next
	var plan:=_earned_dynasty_prepare_second(source,first)
	if not plan.has("wire") or not Codec.same(plan.wire.event,next.event): return null
	if not next.has("opening"):
		return plan.season if Codec.same(plan.wire.season,next.season) else null
	if not _earned_dynasty_second_round_wire(next): return null
	var base: SeasonState=plan.season
	var witness: Dictionary=next.season.physical.pending if next.opening.is_empty() else next.opening
	base.physical.pending=witness.duplicate(true)
	var opening:=_earned_dynasty_opening_plan(base)
	if opening.is_empty(): return null
	var result: SeasonState=base if next.opening.is_empty() else opening.candidate
	# The same native Shop reducer accepts one command at a time; no
	# materialized buyer, cost, inventory, wallet or visit is authoritative.
	for operation: Variant in next.get("shop",[]):
		if not operation is Dictionary: return null
		result=_installed_earned_dynasty_shop_step(result,operation)
		if result==null: return null
	if next.has("regular"):
		var stages: Dictionary={"dynasty":next.season,"dynasty_regular":next.regular,
			"dynasty_postseason":next.has("playoffs"),"dynasty_playoffs":next.get("playoffs",[])}
		result=_earned_dynasty_replay_regular(result,stages)
		if result==null: return null
		if stages.dynasty_postseason:
			result=_earned_dynasty_replay_playoffs(result,stages)
			if result==null: return null
	if not Codec.same(Adapter.capture("dynasty",result),next.season): return null
	if next.has("close"):
		var close:=_earned_dynasty_second_close_record(source,result)
		if close.is_empty() or not Codec.same(close,next.close): return null
	return result


static func _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_round(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,
		image: Dictionary,opening: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if source.dynasty_next.is_empty() or not source.dynasty_next.get("opening",{}).is_empty(): return {}
	var value: Dictionary=Owner._bytes_value(expected)
	if not Codec.whole(value.get("version"),15,16): return {}
	value.version=16
	value.current.dynasty_next.season=image
	value.current.dynasty_next.opening=opening
	return _commit_installed_earned_dynasty_second(source,expected,value)


# Preserve the original birth, opener and first Shop while advancing only
# the active carried regular history. Every record replays through v18.
static func _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_regular(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,
		image: Dictionary,regular: Array,shop_op: Dictionary={}) -> Dictionary:
	var next: Dictionary=source.dynasty_next
	if next.is_empty() or next.get("opening",{}).is_empty(): return {}
	var value: Dictionary=Owner._bytes_value(expected)
	if not Codec.whole(value.get("version"),16,18): return {}
	value.version=18
	value.current.dynasty_next.season=image
	value.current.dynasty_next.shop=next.get("shop",[])
	value.current.dynasty_next.regular=regular
	return _commit_installed_earned_dynasty_second(source,expected,value,shop_op)


# The v19 bracket may extend, but never replace the v18 regular witness.
static func _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_playoffs(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,
		image: Dictionary,playoffs: Array) -> Dictionary:
	var next: Dictionary=source.dynasty_next
	if not next.get("regular") is Array or next.regular.size()!=9: return {}
	var value: Dictionary=Owner._bytes_value(expected)
	if not Codec.whole(value.get("version"),18,19): return {}
	value.version=19
	value.current.dynasty_next.season=image
	value.current.dynasty_next.playoffs=playoffs
	return _commit_installed_earned_dynasty_second(source,expected,value)


# Shop is the only warm incremental proof here. The current revision and
# all earlier rounds were already authenticated before opening the shop.
# The cold reader still reconstructs the whole history, including this op.
static func _verify_carried_shop_append(before: Dictionary,after: Dictionary,
		prior: SeasonState,command: Dictionary) -> SeasonState:
	if prior==null or not _installed_earned_dynasty_shop_window(prior) \
			or not Codec.same(before.event,after.event) or not Codec.same(before.opening,after.opening): return null
	var old_log: Array
	var new_log: Array
	if prior.round_index==1:
		if before.has("regular") or after.has("regular"): return null
		old_log=before.get("shop",[])
		new_log=after.shop
	else:
		if not before.has("regular") or not after.has("regular") or not Codec.same(before.get("shop",[]),after.get("shop",[])): return null
		var old_rounds: Array=before.regular
		var new_rounds: Array=after.regular
		if old_rounds.size()!=prior.round_index-1 or new_rounds.size()!=old_rounds.size() \
				or not Codec.same(old_rounds.slice(0,-1),new_rounds.slice(0,-1)): return null
		var old_row: Dictionary=old_rounds.back()
		var new_row: Dictionary=new_rounds.back()
		if not Codec.same(old_row.human,new_row.human) or old_row.cpu!=new_row.cpu: return null
		old_log=old_row.shop
		new_log=new_row.shop
	if new_log.size()!=old_log.size()+1 or not Codec.same(new_log.slice(0,-1),old_log) \
			or not Codec.same(new_log.back(),command): return null
	var replay:=_installed_earned_dynasty_shop_step(prior,new_log.back())
	if replay==null: return null
	var captured:=Adapter.capture("dynasty",replay)
	if captured.is_empty() or not Codec.same(captured,after.season): return null
	for field: String in ["state","opponents","physical","ownership"]:
		if not Codec.same(captured.get(field),before.season.get(field)): return null
	return replay


static func _commit_installed_earned_dynasty_second(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,
		value: Dictionary,shop_op: Dictionary={}) -> Dictionary:
	var encoded:=_native_materialize(value)
	if encoded.is_empty(): return {}
	var body: PackedByteArray=encoded.bytes
	var checked:=_installed_dugout_d1_parts(encoded.value)
	if checked.is_empty() or not Codec.same(checked.dynasty_next.event,source.dynasty_next.event) \
			or not Codec.same(checked.dynasty,source.dynasty) or not Codec.same(checked.dynasty_close,source.dynasty_close) \
			or not Codec.same(checked.current,source.current) or not Codec.same(checked.head,source.head): return {}
	if value.version>=18 and (not Codec.same(checked.dynasty_next.opening,source.dynasty_next.opening) \
			or not Codec.same(checked.dynasty_next.shop,source.dynasty_next.get("shop",[]))): return {}
	if value.version>=19 and not Codec.same(checked.dynasty_next.regular,source.dynasty_next.regular): return {}
	if value.version==20 and (source.dynasty_next.has("close") \
			or not Codec.same(checked.dynasty_next.season,source.dynasty_next.season) \
			or not Codec.same(checked.dynasty_next.get("playoffs"),source.dynasty_next.get("playoffs"))): return {}
	var first: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty
	var second: SeasonState
	if shop_op.is_empty(): second=_earned_dynasty_verified_second(checked,first)
	else: second=_verify_carried_shop_append(source.dynasty_next,checked.dynasty_next,
		_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_next,shop_op)
	if second==null: return {}
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages: return {}
	var ordinary: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	_invalidate()
	_installed_dugout_d1_bound={"bytes":body,"page":source.pin,"draft":ordinary,"dynasty":first,"dynasty_next":second}
	return installed_dugout_d1_view(body)


# Each settled row keeps the original human proof and just a count of the
# immutable CPU envelopes consumed from the canonical session. A pending
# round is never represented as a settled row, and its Shop cannot open.
static func _earned_dynasty_regular_wire(rows: Variant,image: Dictionary,pending: Dictionary,playoffs: Variant=[]) -> bool:
	if not rows is Array or rows.size()>9 or not image.physical.get("reports") is Array: return false
	var report_count: int=2
	for i: int in rows.size():
		var row: Variant=rows[i]
		if not Codec.keys(row,["human","cpu","shop"]) or not row.human is Array or row.human.size()!=13 \
				or not Codec.whole(row.human[0],(i+1)*3,(i+1)*3) or not row.shop is Array or row.shop.size()>128 \
				or not Codec.whole(row.cpu,2,5 if i==8 else 2): return false
		report_count+=int(row.cpu)
	if not playoffs is Array or playoffs.size()>2: return false
	for i: int in playoffs.size():
		var row: Variant=playoffs[i]
		if not Codec.keys(row,["human","cpu","shop"]) or not Codec.whole(row.cpu,1 if i==0 else 0,2 if i==0 else 0): return false
		report_count+=int(row.cpu)
	if report_count!=image.physical.reports.size(): return false
	var round: int=rows.size()+1
	if not Codec.whole(image.state.get("round_index"),round,round): return false
	if pending.is_empty(): return not rows.is_empty()
	return round<10 and _earned_dynasty_regular_pending(pending,round)


static func _earned_dynasty_pending_fields(pending: Dictionary) -> bool:
	if not pending.has("property"): return Codec.keys(pending,["human","reports"])
	return Codec.keys(pending,["human","reports","property"]) and BaseArchive._digest(pending.property)


# The settled journal stores no second copy of the stadium or its key.
# Reconstruct it from the already proved season and exact scheduled fixture.
static func _earned_dynasty_pending_witness(season: SeasonState,human: Array,reports: Array) -> Dictionary:
	var pending: Dictionary={"human":human.duplicate(true),"reports":reports.duplicate(true)}
	var key: String=Owner.WorkshopApply.property_key(season,season.pending_fixture())
	if not key.is_empty(): pending.property=key
	return pending


# Postseason uses the saved bracket, not regular fixture arithmetic. A
# losing semifinal may need a separate neutral CPU final before payment.
static func _earned_dynasty_playoff_wire(rows: Variant,image: Dictionary,pending: Dictionary) -> bool:
	if not rows is Array or rows.size()>2 or (rows.is_empty() and pending.is_empty()): return false
	var phase: Variant=image.state.get("phase")
	for i: int in rows.size():
		var row: Variant=rows[i]
		if not Codec.keys(row,["human","cpu","shop"]) or not row.human is Array or row.human.size()!=13 \
				or not Codec.whole(row.human[0],30 if i==0 else 32,31 if i==0 else 32) \
				or not row.shop is Array or row.shop.size()>128: return false
	# Serialized native JSON numbers restore as floats; strict array
	# membership tests against integer enums do not accept those values.
	if pending.is_empty(): return Codec.whole(phase,SeasonState.Phase.FINAL,SeasonState.Phase.COMPLETE)
	if rows.size()>1 or not _earned_dynasty_pending_fields(pending) or not pending.human is Array \
			or pending.human.size()!=13 or not pending.reports is Array: return false
	return (rows.is_empty() and phase==SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL and Codec.whole(pending.human[0],30,31) and pending.reports.size()<=2) \
		or (rows.size()==1 and phase==SeasonState.Phase.FINAL and Codec.whole(pending.human[0],32,32) and pending.reports.is_empty())


static func _earned_dynasty_playoff_ready(season: SeasonState) -> bool:
	if not Adapter.scope("dynasty",season) or season.round_index!=10 or season.picks.size()!=4 or not season.playoff_seeds.has(0) \
			or season.physical.cursor!=season.physical.reports.size() or season.champion!=-1: return false
	var f: Dictionary=season.pending_fixture()
	if f.get("home")!=0 and f.get("away")!=0: return false
	if season.phase==SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL:
		return season.results.size()==30 and season.player_results.size()==10 and season.physical.reports.size()==20 \
			and f.get("id") in [30,31] and f.get("round")==10
	return season.phase==SeasonState.Phase.FINAL and season.results.size()==32 and season.player_results.size()==11 \
		and season.physical.reports.size()==21 and f.get("id")==32 and f.get("round")==11 and f.get("neutral")==true


static func _earned_dynasty_playoff_plan(season: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	if not _earned_dynasty_playoff_ready(season): return {}
	var f: Dictionary=season.pending_fixture()
	var pending: Dictionary=season.physical.pending
	if not _earned_dynasty_pending_fields(pending) or not pending.human is Array or pending.human.size()!=13 \
			or not Codec.whole(pending.human[0],int(f.id),int(f.id)) or not pending.reports is Array: return {}
	var semi: bool=season.phase==SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL
	var count: int=pending.reports.size()
	if count>(2 if semi else 0): return {}
	var other: int=61-int(f.id)
	for i: int in count:
		var row: Variant=pending.reports[i]
		if not row is Dictionary or not Codec.whole(row.get("fixture"),other if i==0 else 32,other if i==0 else 32): return {}
	var plan: Dictionary=Adapter.DynastyPending.project(season)
	if plan.has("error") or not plan.get("candidate") is SeasonState: return {}
	if plan.has("fixture"):
		var next: Dictionary=plan.fixture
		return plan if semi and count<2 and next.get("id")== (other if count==0 else 32) \
			and next.get("round")==10+count and next.get("away")!=0 and next.get("home")!=0 else {}
	var after: SeasonState=plan.candidate
	if after.round_index!=10 or not after.physical.pending.is_empty() or after.physical.cursor!=after.physical.reports.size() \
			or after.player_results.size()!=season.player_results.size()+1 or after.results.size()!=season.results.size()+count+1 \
			or not Codec.same(after.results.slice(0,season.results.size()),season.results) \
			or not Codec.same(after.player_results.slice(0,season.player_results.size()),season.player_results) \
			or not Codec.same(after.physical.reports.slice(0,season.physical.reports.size()),season.physical.reports) \
			or not Codec.same(after.physical.reports.slice(season.physical.reports.size()),pending.reports): return {}
	var human: Dictionary=after.player_results.back()
	if human.get("id")!=f.id or not Codec.same([human.away_runs,human.home_runs],pending.human.slice(1,3)): return {}
	var win: bool=SeasonState._winner(human)==0
	if semi and win: return plan if count==1 and _earned_dynasty_playoff_ready(after) else {}
	if semi: return plan if count==2 and after.phase==SeasonState.Phase.COMPLETE and after.champion>0 else {}
	return plan if after.phase==SeasonState.Phase.COMPLETE and after.champion==SeasonState._winner(human) else {}


static func _earned_dynasty_replay_playoffs(start: SeasonState,source: Dictionary) -> SeasonState:
	var current: SeasonState=start
	var stored: Array=source.dynasty.physical.reports
	for row: Dictionary in source.dynasty_playoffs:
		var offset: int=current.physical.reports.size()
		current.physical.pending=_earned_dynasty_pending_witness(current,row.human,stored.slice(offset,offset+int(row.cpu)))
		var plan:=_earned_dynasty_playoff_plan(current)
		if plan.is_empty() or plan.has("fixture"): return null
		current=plan.candidate
		for op: Variant in row.shop:
			if not op is Dictionary: return null
			current=_installed_earned_dynasty_shop_step(current,op)
			if current==null: return null
	if not source.dynasty.physical.pending.is_empty():
		current.physical.pending=source.dynasty.physical.pending.duplicate(true)
		if _earned_dynasty_playoff_plan(current).is_empty(): return null
	return current


static func _earned_dynasty_regular_pending(pending: Dictionary,round: int) -> bool:
	var id: int=round*3
	if not _earned_dynasty_pending_fields(pending) or not pending.human is Array or pending.human.size()!=13 \
			or not Codec.whole(pending.human[0],id,id) or not pending.reports is Array \
			or pending.reports.size()>(5 if round==9 else 2): return false
	for i: int in pending.reports.size():
		var row: Variant=pending.reports[i]
		if not row is Dictionary or not Codec.whole(row.get("fixture"),id+i+1,id+i+1): return false
	return true


static func _earned_dynasty_regular_ready(season: SeasonState) -> bool:
	if not Adapter.scope("dynasty",season) or season.phase!=SeasonState.Phase.REGULAR \
			or season.round_index<1 or season.round_index>=10 or season.picks.size()!=4 \
			or season.player_results.size()!=season.round_index or season.results.size()!=season.round_index*3 \
			or season.physical.reports.size()!=season.round_index*2 \
			or season.physical.cursor!=season.physical.reports.size(): return false
	var f: Dictionary=season.pending_fixture()
	return f.get("id")==season.round_index*3 and f.get("round")==season.round_index \
		and (f.get("away")==0 or f.get("home")==0)


static func _earned_dynasty_regular_plan(season: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	if not _earned_dynasty_regular_ready(season) or not _earned_dynasty_regular_pending(season.physical.pending,season.round_index): return {}
	var round: int=season.round_index
	var witness: Dictionary=season.physical.pending
	var count: int=witness.reports.size()
	var plan: Dictionary=Adapter.DynastyPending.project(season)
	if plan.has("error") or not plan.get("candidate") is SeasonState: return {}
	if plan.has("fixture"):
		var f: Dictionary=plan.fixture
		var next: int=round*3+count+1
		var expected_round: int=round if next<30 else (10 if next<32 else 11)
		return plan if count<(5 if round==9 else 2) and f.get("id")==next and f.get("round")==expected_round \
			and f.get("home")!=0 and f.get("away")!=0 else {}
	var after: SeasonState=plan.candidate
	if after.round_index!=round+1 or not after.physical.pending.is_empty() \
			or after.player_results.size()!=round+1 or after.results.size()!=round*3+count+1 \
			or after.physical.cursor!=after.physical.reports.size() \
			or not Codec.same(after.results.slice(0,round*3),season.results) \
			or not Codec.same(after.player_results.slice(0,round),season.player_results) \
			or not Codec.same(after.physical.reports.slice(0,round*2),season.physical.reports) \
			or not Codec.same(after.physical.reports.slice(round*2),witness.reports): return {}
	var human: Dictionary=after.player_results.back()
	if human.get("id")!=round*3 or not Codec.same([human.away_runs,human.home_runs],witness.human.slice(1,3)): return {}
	if round<9:
		if count!=2 or not _earned_dynasty_regular_ready(after): return {}
	elif not ((count==2 and after.phase==SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL and after.playoff_seeds.has(0)) \
			or (count==5 and after.phase==SeasonState.Phase.COMPLETE and not after.playoff_seeds.has(0))): return {}
	return plan


static func _earned_dynasty_replay_regular(start: SeasonState,source: Dictionary) -> SeasonState:
	var current: SeasonState=start
	var stored: Array=source.dynasty.physical.reports
	for row: Dictionary in source.dynasty_regular:
		var offset: int=current.physical.reports.size()
		current.physical.pending=_earned_dynasty_pending_witness(current,row.human,stored.slice(offset,offset+int(row.cpu)))
		var plan:=_earned_dynasty_regular_plan(current)
		if plan.is_empty() or plan.has("fixture"): return null
		current=plan.candidate
		for op: Variant in row.shop:
			if not op is Dictionary: return null
			current=_installed_earned_dynasty_shop_step(current,op)
			if current==null: return null
	if not source.dynasty_postseason and not source.dynasty.physical.pending.is_empty():
		current.physical.pending=source.dynasty.physical.pending.duplicate(true)
		if _earned_dynasty_regular_plan(current).is_empty(): return null
	return current


static func _earned_dynasty_home_pending(pending: Dictionary) -> bool:
	return Codec.keys(pending,["human","reports"]) and pending.human is Array and pending.human.size()==13 \
		and Codec.whole(pending.human[0],3,3) and pending.reports is Array and pending.reports.is_empty()

static func _earned_dynasty_home_plan(season: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	if season==null or season.phase!=SeasonState.Phase.REGULAR or season.round_index!=1 \
			or season.results.size()!=3 or season.player_results.size()!=1 or season.physical.reports.size()!=2 \
			or not _earned_dynasty_home_pending(season.physical.pending): return {}
	var fixture: Dictionary=season.pending_fixture()
	if fixture.get("id")!=3 or fixture.get("home")!=0 or fixture.get("away")!=4: return {}
	var plan: Dictionary=Adapter.DynastyPending.project(season)
	return plan if not plan.has("error") and plan.get("fixture") is Dictionary and plan.fixture.get("id")==4 else {}

static func _earned_dynasty_opening_pending(pending: Dictionary) -> bool:
	if not _earned_dynasty_pending_fields(pending) or not pending.human is Array or pending.human.size()!=13 \
			or not Codec.whole(pending.human[0],0,0) or not pending.reports is Array or pending.reports.size()>2: return false
	for i: int in pending.reports.size():
		var row: Variant=pending.reports[i]
		if not row is Dictionary or not Codec.whole(row.get("fixture"),i+1,i+1): return false
	return true

static func _earned_dynasty_opening_plan(season: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	if season==null or season.phase!=SeasonState.Phase.REGULAR or season.round_index!=0 or season.picks.size()!=4 \
			or not season.results.is_empty() or not season.player_results.is_empty() or not season.physical.reports.is_empty() \
			or not _earned_dynasty_opening_pending(season.physical.pending): return {}
	var plan: Dictionary=Adapter.DynastyPending.project(season)
	if plan.has("error") or not plan.get("candidate") is SeasonState: return {}
	var count: int=season.physical.pending.reports.size()
	if count<2:
		return plan if plan.get("fixture") is Dictionary and plan.fixture.get("id")==count+1 else {}
	return plan if not plan.has("fixture") and _earned_dynasty_opening_settled(plan.candidate,season.physical.pending) else {}

static func _earned_dynasty_opening_settled(season: SeasonState,witness: Dictionary) -> bool:
	if season.round_index!=1 or season.phase!=SeasonState.Phase.REGULAR or season.results.size()!=3 \
			or season.player_results.size()!=1 or not season.physical.pending.is_empty() \
			or not Codec.same(season.physical.reports,witness.reports): return false
	var played: Dictionary=season.player_results[0]
	if played.get("id")!=0 or played.get("away_runs")!=witness.human[1] \
			or played.get("home_runs")!=witness.human[2]: return false
	var expected: Array[Dictionary]=[{}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}]
	for i: int in range(3):
		var row: Dictionary=season.results[i]
		if row.get("id")!=i: return false
		var winner: int=SeasonState._winner(row)
		for club: int in [int(row.away),int(row.home)]: expected[club][str(i)]=winner==club
	for club: int in range(6):
		var build: SeasonBuild=season.build if club==0 else season.opponents.clubs.get(str(club),{}).get("build")
		if build==null or build._bank==null or not Codec.same(build._bank.view().rewards,expected[club]): return false
	return true

static func installed_dugout_d1_view(expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	if not expected.is_empty() and expected in [_installed_equipment_bound,_installed_named_base_bound]:
		var named:=_bound_installed_named_game(expected)
		if named.is_empty(): return {}
		return _installed_named_base_view(expected,named.named_base,named.draft) if named.has("named_base") \
			else _installed_equipment_view(expected,named.equipment,named.draft)
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if source.is_empty(): return {}
	if not source.dynasty_third.is_empty():
		var third: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_third
		var closed: bool=source.dynasty_third.has("close")
		var close: Dictionary=source.dynasty_third.close if closed else _earned_dynasty_active_preceding_close(source)
		if close.is_empty(): return {}
		var pending: Dictionary=third.physical.pending
		var reports: int=pending.get("reports",[]).size()
		var played: int=third.round_index
		var waiting: bool=not pending.is_empty()
		var state: String="dynasty_pending" if waiting else ("dynasty_third_ready" if played==0 else "dynasty_paid")
		var payable: bool=waiting and reports==2
		var total: int=2
		var post: String=""
		if third.phase==SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL: post="semifinal"
		elif third.phase==SeasonState.Phase.FINAL: post="final"
		elif third.phase==SeasonState.Phase.COMPLETE:
			var managed: int=third.player_results.size()
			post="champion" if third.champion==0 else ("runner_up" if managed==12 else ("semifinal_exit" if managed==11 else "eliminated"))
		if not waiting and played==10:
			state="dynasty_postseason_ready" if _earned_dynasty_playoff_ready(third) else "dynasty_regular_done"
		if waiting and played==10:
			var projection:=_earned_dynasty_playoff_plan(third)
			if projection.is_empty(): return {}
			payable=not projection.has("fixture")
			var home: bool=third.pending_fixture().get("home")==0
			var won: bool=(pending.human[2]>pending.human[1])==home
			total=0 if post=="final" else (1 if won else 2)
		if waiting and played==9 and reports>=2:
			var tail:=_earned_dynasty_regular_plan(third)
			if tail.is_empty(): return {}
			payable=not tail.has("fixture")
			total=2 if payable and reports==2 else 5
		var workshop: Dictionary={}
		var chair: Dictionary=source.dynasty_third.get("workshop_chair",source.dynasty_inherited_chair)
		if closed:
			var first: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty
			var owner:=_earned_dynasty_workshop_owner(close,int(first.career.start_basis.genesis.genesis.seed),third)
			if owner==null: return {}
			owner.workshop_chair_purchase=chair
			var balance: int=Owner.WorkshopPurchase.available_balance(owner)
			var drafted: bool=source.dynasty_third.has("workshop_layout") or not source.dynasty_inherited_layout.is_empty()
			var applied: bool=source.dynasty_third.has("workshop_applied") or not source.dynasty_inherited_application.is_empty()
			workshop={"balance":balance,"owned":not chair.is_empty(),"price":Owner.WorkshopPurchase.PRICE,
				"can_buy":chair.is_empty() and balance>=Owner.WorkshopPurchase.PRICE,
				"drafted":drafted,"can_draft":not chair.is_empty() and not drafted,"applied":applied,
				"can_apply":drafted and not applied and owner.ordinal<ClubCareer.MAX_RUNS}
		return {"bytes":expected.duplicate(),"state":state,"ordinal":source.dynasty_compact_ordinal,
			"picks":third.picks.duplicate(),"credits":int(close.common.aggregate.currency)-(0 if chair.is_empty() else Owner.WorkshopPurchase.PRICE),
			"workshop":workshop,
			"cash":third.cash(),"plaques":close.common.aggregate.plaques.size(),"round":maxi(1,played+int(waiting)),"postseason":post,
			"score":pending.human.slice(1,3) if waiting else [],"reports":reports,"cpu_total":total,
			"settle_available":payable,
			"allow_play_next":not third.build.pack_pending() and ((state=="dynasty_paid" and _earned_dynasty_regular_ready(third)) or state=="dynasty_postseason_ready"),
			"shop_available":state in ["dynasty_paid","dynasty_postseason_ready"] and _installed_earned_dynasty_shop_window(third),
			"results":third.results.slice(maxi(0,(played-1)*3),played*3).duplicate(true) if state=="dynasty_paid" else [],
			"dynasty_closed":closed,"close_available":not closed and _earned_dynasty_close_boundary(third),
			"completion":close.local.receipt.duplicate(true) if closed else {}}
	var draft: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	if not source.dynasty.is_empty():
		var dynasty: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty
		if not source.dynasty_next.is_empty():
			var second: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_next
			var pending_second: Dictionary=second.physical.pending
			var played: int=second.round_index
			var waiting_second: bool=not pending_second.is_empty()
			var state_second: String="dynasty_pending" if waiting_second else ("dynasty_paid" if played>0 else "dynasty_second_ready")
			if played==10 and not waiting_second:
				state_second="dynasty_postseason_ready" if _earned_dynasty_playoff_ready(second) else "dynasty_regular_done"
			var count_second: int=pending_second.get("reports",[]).size()
			var total_second: int=2
			var payable: bool=waiting_second and count_second==2
			if waiting_second and played==9 and count_second>=2:
				var tail:=_earned_dynasty_regular_plan(second)
				if tail.is_empty(): return {}
				payable=not tail.has("fixture")
				total_second=2 if payable and count_second==2 else 5
			var post_second: String=""
			if second.phase==SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL: post_second="semifinal"
			elif second.phase==SeasonState.Phase.FINAL: post_second="final"
			elif second.phase==SeasonState.Phase.COMPLETE:
				var managed: int=second.player_results.size()
				post_second="champion" if second.champion==0 else ("runner_up" if managed==12 else ("semifinal_exit" if managed==11 else "eliminated"))
			var score_second: Array=pending_second.human.slice(1,3) if waiting_second else []
			if waiting_second and played==10:
				var bracket:=_earned_dynasty_playoff_plan(second)
				if bracket.is_empty(): return {}
				payable=not bracket.has("fixture")
				var home: bool=second.pending_fixture().get("home")==0
				var won: bool=(score_second[1]>score_second[0])==home
				total_second=0 if post_second=="final" else (1 if won else 2)
			var next_ready: bool=(state_second=="dynasty_paid" and _earned_dynasty_regular_ready(second)) or state_second=="dynasty_postseason_ready"
			var close_second: Dictionary=source.dynasty_next.get("close",{})
			var credits_second: int=int(close_second.common.aggregate.currency if not close_second.is_empty() else source.dynasty_close.common.aggregate.currency)
			return {"bytes":expected.duplicate(),"state":state_second,"picks":second.picks.duplicate(),
				"credits":credits_second,"cash":second.cash(),"ordinal":2,
				"round":maxi(1,played+int(waiting_second)),"postseason":post_second,
				"dynasty_closed":not close_second.is_empty(),
				"close_available":close_second.is_empty() and _earned_dynasty_close_boundary(second),
				"completion":close_second.common.last_fold.receipt.duplicate(true) if not close_second.is_empty() else {},
				"allow_play_next":next_ready and not second.build.pack_pending(),
				"shop_available":next_ready and _installed_earned_dynasty_shop_window(second),"reports":count_second,
				"results":second.results.slice((played-1)*3,played*3).duplicate(true) if state_second=="dynasty_paid" else [],
				"cpu_total":total_second,"settle_available":payable,"score":score_second}
		var waiting: bool=not dynasty.physical.pending.is_empty()
		var score: Array=dynasty.physical.pending.human.slice(1,3) if waiting else []
		var state: String="dynasty_draft"
		if waiting: state="dynasty_pending"
		elif not source.dynasty_round_one.is_empty():
			state="dynasty_paid" if dynasty.phase==SeasonState.Phase.REGULAR else ("dynasty_postseason_ready" if _earned_dynasty_playoff_ready(dynasty) else "dynasty_regular_done")
		var count: int=dynasty.physical.pending.get("reports",[]).size() if waiting else 0
		var total: int=2
		var ready_to_pay: bool=waiting and count==2
		if waiting and dynasty.round_index==9 and count>=2:
			var tail:=_earned_dynasty_regular_plan(dynasty)
			if tail.is_empty(): return {}
			ready_to_pay=not tail.has("fixture")
			total=2 if ready_to_pay and count==2 else 5
		var post: String=""
		if dynasty.phase==SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL: post="semifinal"
		elif dynasty.phase==SeasonState.Phase.FINAL: post="final"
		elif dynasty.phase==SeasonState.Phase.COMPLETE:
			var managed: int=dynasty.player_results.size()
			post="champion" if dynasty.champion==0 else ("runner_up" if managed==12 else ("semifinal_exit" if managed==11 else "eliminated"))
		if waiting and dynasty.round_index==10:
			var playoff_plan:=_earned_dynasty_playoff_plan(dynasty)
			if playoff_plan.is_empty(): return {}
			ready_to_pay=not playoff_plan.has("fixture")
			var managed: Dictionary=dynasty.pending_fixture()
			var won: bool=(score[1]>score[0])==(managed.get("home")==0)
			total=0 if post=="final" else (1 if won else 2)
		var banked: Dictionary=source.dynasty_close
		var credits: int=int(source.current.common.aggregate.currency) if banked.is_empty() else int(banked.common.aggregate.currency)
		return {"bytes":expected.duplicate(),"state":state,"picks":dynasty.picks.duplicate(),
			"offers":dynasty.offers() if dynasty.phase==SeasonState.Phase.DRAFT else [],
			"credits":credits,"cash":dynasty.cash(),"score":score,
			"round":dynasty.round_index+int(waiting),"reports":count,"cpu_total":total,"settle_available":ready_to_pay,
			"shop_available":_installed_earned_dynasty_shop_window(dynasty),
			"results":dynasty.results.slice(maxi(0,dynasty.results.size()-3)).duplicate(true) if state=="dynasty_paid" else [],
			"postseason":post,"dynasty_closed":not banked.is_empty(),
			"close_available":banked.is_empty() and _earned_dynasty_close_boundary(dynasty),
			"completion":banked.common.last_fold.receipt.duplicate(true) if not banked.is_empty() else {}}
	if source.tier in [2,3]:
		var fitted: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d2.gd")
		if fitted.installed_fitted_tier(draft)!=source.tier: return {}
		var d2_state: String="draft" if draft.picks.size()<4 else fitted.installed_boundary(draft)
		if d2_state.is_empty(): return {}
		var earned_d1: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd")
		var finish_d2: String=earned_d1.final_regular_destination(draft)
		if finish_d2.is_empty(): finish_d2=earned_d1.settled_semifinal(draft)
		if finish_d2.is_empty(): finish_d2=earned_d1.managed_final_destination(draft)
		var semi_d2: bool=d2_state=="semifinal_unpaid"
		var post_d2: bool=finish_d2 in ["final_ready","semifinal_exit","final_champion","final_runner_up"]
		var count_d2: int=draft.physical.pending.reports.size() if d2_state in ["unpaid","semifinal_unpaid"] else 0
		var total_d2: int=earned_d1.semifinal_jobs(draft) if semi_d2 else (0 if d2_state=="final_unpaid" else 2)
		if d2_state=="unpaid" and draft.round_index==_installed_dugout_d1_regular_limit()-1 and count_d2>=2:
			var pending_d2:=_installed_dugout_d1_pending(expected)
			if pending_d2.is_empty(): return {}
			if pending_d2.projection.has("fixture") or count_d2>2: total_d2=5
		var close_d2: String=earned_d1.close_destination(draft) if d2_state=="paid" else ""
		var completion_d2: Dictionary={}
		if source.closed:
			if close_d2.is_empty(): return {}
			completion_d2=source.current.ordinary_state.last_close.receipt.duplicate(true)
		var d2_scores: Array=[]
		if d2_state=="paid":
			var start_d2: int=30 if post_d2 else (draft.results.size()-3 if finish_d2.is_empty() else 27)
			for row: Dictionary in draft.results.slice(start_d2):
				d2_scores.append([int(row.id),int(row.away_runs),int(row.home_runs)])
		return {"bytes":expected.duplicate(),"tier":source.tier,"picks":draft.picks.duplicate(),"offers":draft.offers(),
			"credits":int(source.current.common.aggregate.currency),"cash":draft.cash(),"state":d2_state,"closed":source.closed,
			"close_available":not source.closed and not close_d2.is_empty(),"completion":completion_d2,
			"d3_title_banked":source.tier==3 and source.closed and close_d2=="champion" and completion_d2.get("finish")=="champion" \
				and source.current.common.aggregate.titles.get("dugout:3")==true \
				and preload("res://src/gameplay/season/league_unlocks.gd").from_titles(source.current.common.aggregate.titles).has("dynasty"),
			"start_d3_available":source.tier==2 and source.closed and close_d2=="champion" and completion_d2.get("finish")=="champion",
			"start_scramble_available":source.tier==2 and source.closed and close_d2=="champion" \
				and completion_d2.get("finish")=="champion" \
				and "scramble" in EarnedNamedBase.Unlocks.from_titles(source.current.common.aggregate.titles),
			"cpu_reports":count_d2,"cpu_total":total_d2,"finish":finish_d2,
			"scores":d2_scores,"shop_available":d2_state=="paid" and not source.closed and draft.shop_available(),
			"round":draft.round_index+int(d2_state in ["unpaid","ready"]),
			"managed_semifinal_id":int(draft.player_results[10].id) if post_d2 else -1,
			"next_available":not source.closed and preload("earned_dugout_d2.gd").next_installed_available(draft)}
	var state: String="draft"
	if draft.picks.size()==4:
		if not draft.physical.pending.is_empty(): state="unpaid"
		else: state="ready" if draft.round_index==0 else "paid"
	var earned: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd")
	if earned.semifinal_pending(draft): state="semifinal_unpaid"
	elif earned.managed_final_pending(draft): state="final_unpaid"
	var reports: int=draft.physical.pending.reports.size() if state in ["unpaid","semifinal_unpaid"] else 0
	var finish: String=earned.final_regular_destination(draft)
	if finish.is_empty(): finish=earned.settled_semifinal(draft)
	if finish.is_empty(): finish=earned.managed_final_destination(draft)
	var completion: Dictionary={}
	if source.closed:
		if state!="paid" or earned.close_destination(draft).is_empty(): return {}
		completion=source.current.ordinary_state.last_close.receipt.duplicate(true)
	var cpu_total: int=earned.semifinal_jobs(draft) if state=="semifinal_unpaid" else (0 if state=="final_unpaid" else 2)
	if state=="unpaid" and draft.round_index==_installed_dugout_d1_regular_limit()-1 and reports>=2:
		var plan:=_installed_dugout_d1_pending(expected)
		if plan.is_empty(): return {}
		if plan.projection.has("fixture") or reports>2: cpu_total=5
	var scores: Array=[]
	var playoff_club_id: int=-1
	if state=="paid":
		var settled_semi: bool=finish in ["semifinal_exit","final_ready","final_champion","final_runner_up"]
		if settled_semi: playoff_club_id=int(draft.player_results[10].id)
		var start: int=30 if settled_semi else (27 if not finish.is_empty() else draft.results.size()-3)
		for row: Dictionary in draft.results.slice(start):
			scores.append([int(row.id),int(row.away_runs),int(row.home_runs)])
	return {"bytes":expected.duplicate(),"picks":draft.picks.duplicate(),"offers":draft.offers(),
		"credits":int(source.current.common.aggregate.currency),"cash":draft.cash(),"state":state,
		"cpu_reports":reports,"cpu_total":cpu_total,"scores":scores,"round":draft.round_index if state in ["paid","semifinal_unpaid","final_unpaid"] else draft.round_index+1,
		"next_available":draft.round_index<_installed_dugout_d1_regular_limit(),"finish":finish,"playoff_club_id":playoff_club_id,
		"shop_available":state=="paid" and not source.closed and draft.shop_available(),"closed":source.closed,
		"close_available":not source.closed and state=="paid" and not earned.close_destination(draft).is_empty(),
		"completion":completion,"tier":1,
		"start_d2_available":source.closed and finish=="final_champion" and completion.get("finish")=="champion",
		"start_doubleheader_available":source.closed and finish=="final_champion" and completion.get("finish")=="champion" \
			and "doubleheader" in EarnedNamedBase.Unlocks.from_titles(source.current.common.aggregate.titles)}

# The menu cannot supply a child result, an archive path, or a saved session.
static func adopt_installed_dugout_d1_read(reader: Node, expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	if not is_instance_valid(reader) or reader.get_script()!=load("res://src/gameplay/season/successor_dynasty_owner_v1/installed_dugout_d1_reader.gd") \
			or not reader.verified_revision(expected): return {}
	var source:=_installed_dugout_d1_source()
	if source.is_empty() or source.bytes!=expected: return {}
	if source.has("named_base"):
		var named_draft:=EarnedNamedBase.local_draft(source.named_base,true)
		if named_draft==null or not _matches(expected): return {}
		_invalidate()
		_installed_named_base_bound=expected.duplicate()
		return _installed_named_base_view(expected,source.named_base,named_draft)
	if source.has("equipment"):
		var equipment_draft:=EquipmentBase.local_draft(source.equipment,true)
		if equipment_draft==null or not _matches(expected): return {}
		_invalidate()
		_installed_equipment_bound=expected.duplicate()
		return _installed_equipment_view(expected,source.equipment,equipment_draft)
	var draft: SeasonState
	if source.tier==3: draft=_installed_dugout_d3_local_draft(source.current.ordinary)
	elif source.tier==2: draft=_installed_dugout_d2_local_draft(source.current.ordinary)
	else: draft=_installed_dugout_d1_verified_current(source.current.ordinary)
	if draft==null: return {}
	var earned: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd")
	var witness: String=reader.verified_wallets()
	if earned.needs_wallet_types(draft):
		if not earned.restore_wallet_types(draft,witness) \
				or not Codec.same(Adapter.Ordinary.capture(draft),source.current.ordinary): return {}
	elif not witness.is_empty(): return {}
	# The exact child already proved these receipts from the ordered pages.
	# The parent may materialize integer brackets as floats. Try only the
	# other exact representation, accepting it only if the original request
	# and complete canonical image still pass; never rewrite a saved receipt.
	if source.tier==3 and earned.semifinal_pending(draft) and not draft.physical.pending.reports.is_empty():
		var projected: Dictionary=Adapter.OrdinaryPending.project(draft)
		if projected.get("error")=="The pending round contains an unexpected physical fixture.":
			draft=earned._materialized_semifinal_fixture_types(draft,false)
		if draft==null or _installed_dugout_d1_project_pending(draft).is_empty() \
			or not Codec.same(Adapter.Ordinary.capture(draft),source.current.ordinary): return {}
	var dynasty: SeasonState
	var live_carried: SeasonState
	if not source.dynasty.is_empty():
		# The exact revision-bound native child already replayed this history.
		# For the paid carried opener, hydrate ONLY the parked terminal season.
		# The active season still comes from the native transition and opening
		# replay; direct JSON hydration changes physical request number types.
		if not source.dynasty_next.is_empty() and not source.dynasty_next.get("opening",{}).is_empty():
			var parked: SeasonState=Adapter.restore("dynasty",source.dynasty)
			if _earned_dynasty_close_boundary(parked):
				if source.dynasty_compact_history.is_empty():
					live_carried=_earned_dynasty_verified_second(source,parked)
				else:
					# The revision-bound worker checked this sealed predecessor.
					var parked_second:=Adapter.restore("dynasty",source.dynasty_next.season)
					if _earned_dynasty_close_boundary(parked_second): live_carried=parked_second
				if live_carried!=null: dynasty=parked
		if dynasty==null:
			var verified: Dictionary={}
			dynasty=_installed_earned_dynasty_draft(source,draft,verified)
			if dynasty==null: return {}
			if live_carried==null: live_carried=verified.get("second")
	if not source.dynasty_next.is_empty() and live_carried==null: return {}
	var live_third: SeasonState
	if not source.dynasty_third.is_empty():
		var plan:=_earned_dynasty_native_active_plan(source,dynasty,live_carried)
		live_third=_earned_dynasty_third_current(plan,source.dynasty_third)
		if live_third==null: return {}
	if not reader.verified_revision(expected): return {}
	_invalidate()
	_installed_dugout_d1_bound={"bytes":expected.duplicate(),"page":source.pin,"draft":draft}
	if dynasty!=null: _installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty=dynasty
	if live_carried!=null: _installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_next=live_carried
	if live_third!=null: _installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_third=live_third
	return installed_dugout_d1_view(expected)

# Called only by the installed bound App after its complete-only prompt.
# The Store derives the receipt from the native settled season. No input
# supplies a score, claim, payout, path, or successor entitlement.
static func complete_installed_dugout_d1(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_shop_app(app) or app.season!=null: return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected)
	if source.is_empty() or source.closed: return {}
	var native: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	var completed: Dictionary=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd").completed_current_for_tier(source.current,native,source.tier)
	if completed.is_empty(): return {}
	var value: Dictionary={"kind":INSTALLED_DUGOUT_D1_KIND,"version":6 if source.tier==3 else (4 if source.tier==2 else 2),"head":source.head,"current":completed}
	if _installed_dugout_d1_parts(value).is_empty(): return {}
	var body:=_native_encoded(value)
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected)
	if body.is_empty() or again.is_empty() or again.closed or again.pages!=source.pages: return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	_invalidate()
	_installed_dugout_d1_bound={"bytes":body,"page":source.pin,"draft":native}
	return installed_dugout_d1_view(body)

# The action receives only the exact live App and uses its verified Store
# binding. New bytes add one close receipt; no earlier history is rewritten.
static func complete_installed_earned_dynasty(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_entry_app(app) or app.season!=null \
			or app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("state")!="dynasty_regular_done" \
			or not app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("close_available",false): return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if source.is_empty() or not source.closed or source.tier!=3 or not source.dynasty_repeatable \
			or source.dynasty.is_empty(): return {}
	if not source.dynasty_third.is_empty():
		return _complete_installed_earned_dynasty_third(source,expected)
	if not source.dynasty_next.is_empty():
		if source.dynasty_next.has("close") or source.dynasty_close.is_empty(): return {}
		var carried: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_next
		var close:=_earned_dynasty_second_close_record(source,carried)
		if close.is_empty(): return {}
		var staged: Dictionary=Owner._bytes_value(expected)
		if not Codec.whole(staged.get("version"),18,19): return {}
		staged.version=20
		staged.current.dynasty_next.close=close
		return _commit_installed_earned_dynasty_second(source,expected,staged)
	if not source.dynasty_close.is_empty(): return {}
	var ordinary: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	var dynasty: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty
	if not _earned_dynasty_close_boundary(dynasty): return {}
	var earned: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d2.gd")
	var birth: Dictionary=earned.dynasty_draft_from_verified(source.current,source.head,ordinary,[])
	var record:=_earned_dynasty_close_record(source,dynasty,birth)
	if record.is_empty(): return {}
	var value: Dictionary={"kind":INSTALLED_DUGOUT_D1_KIND,"version":14,"head":source.head,
		"current":{"active":"dynasty","ordinary_origin":source.current,"dynasty":source.dynasty,
			"round_one":source.dynasty_round_one,"shop":source.dynasty_shop,"regular":source.dynasty_regular,
			"playoffs":source.dynasty_playoffs,"dynasty_close":record}}
	var body:=_native_encoded(value)
	if body.is_empty(): return {}
	var checked:=_installed_dugout_d1_parts(JSON.parse_string(body.get_string_from_utf8()))
	if checked.is_empty() or not Codec.same(checked.dynasty_close,record) \
			or not Codec.same(checked.current,source.current) or not Codec.same(checked.dynasty,source.dynasty): return {}
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected)
	if again.is_empty() or not again.dynasty_close.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages: return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	_invalidate()
	_installed_dugout_d1_bound={"bytes":body,"page":source.pin,"draft":ordinary,"dynasty":dynasty}
	return installed_dugout_d1_view(body)

# An exact warm terminal binding may append only its own Close. The native
# cold reader will replay the season and independently regenerate the receipt.
static func _earned_dynasty_active_preceding_close(source: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if source.dynasty_compact_history.is_empty(): return source.dynasty_next.get("close",{})
	var archived:=_earned_dynasty_archived_compact(source.dynasty_compact_history.back(),source.head)
	return {} if archived.is_empty() else _earned_dynasty_compact_wire(archived).close


static func _complete_installed_earned_dynasty_third(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	if saving or source.dynasty_third.has("close"): return {}
	var before_close:=_earned_dynasty_active_preceding_close(source)
	if before_close.is_empty(): return {}
	var previous: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.duplicate()
	var first: SeasonState=previous.dynasty
	var third: SeasonState=previous.dynasty_third
	var value:=_earned_dynasty_compact_head(Owner._bytes_value(expected))
	if value.is_empty() or value.version==22: return {}
	var active:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(value)
	if not Codec.same(active,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	var record:=_earned_dynasty_third_close_record(before_close, \
		int(first.career.start_basis.genesis.genesis.seed),third,source.dynasty_third.season)
	if record.is_empty(): return {}
	active.close=record
	if value.version==21: value.version=22
	var staged:=_native_materialize(value)
	if staged.is_empty() or _earned_dynasty_compact_head(staged.value).is_empty() \
			or not Codec.same(_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(staged.value).close,record): return {}
	var restored: Dictionary=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(staged.value).duplicate()
	restored.erase("close")
	if not Codec.same(restored,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages or again.pin!=source.pin \
			or not Codec.same(again.dynasty_third,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(staged.bytes,expected)
	saving=false
	return _bind_installed_earned_dynasty_third(staged.bytes,previous,third) if saved else {}


static func buy_installed_earned_dynasty_workshop_chair(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_entry_app(app) or app.season!=null \
			or not app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("dynasty_closed",false): return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if source.is_empty() or not source.dynasty_third.has("close") \
			or source.dynasty_third.has("workshop_chair") or not source.dynasty_inherited_chair.is_empty(): return {}
	var prior: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.duplicate()
	var first: SeasonState=prior.dynasty
	var current: SeasonState=prior.dynasty_third
	var seed_value: int=int(first.career.start_basis.genesis.genesis.seed)
	var owner:=_earned_dynasty_workshop_owner(source.dynasty_third.close,seed_value,current)
	if owner==null: return {}
	var receipt: Dictionary=Owner.WorkshopPurchase.issue(owner)
	if receipt.is_empty(): return {}
	var value:=_earned_dynasty_compact_head(Owner._bytes_value(expected))
	if value.is_empty(): return {}
	var wire:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(value)
	if not Codec.same(wire,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	wire.workshop_chair=receipt
	var staged:=_native_materialize(value)
	if staged.is_empty() or _earned_dynasty_compact_head(staged.value).is_empty(): return {}
	var unchanged:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(staged.value).duplicate()
	if not _earned_dynasty_workshop_receipt_valid(unchanged.close,seed_value,current,unchanged.workshop_chair): return {}
	unchanged.erase("workshop_chair")
	if not Codec.same(unchanged,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages or again.pin!=source.pin \
			or not Codec.same(again.dynasty_compact_history,source.dynasty_compact_history): return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(staged.bytes,expected)
	saving=false
	return _bind_installed_earned_dynasty_third(staged.bytes,prior,current) if saved else {}


# Draft-only: proof of a paid receiver is retained without changing any
# stadium, schedule, wallet, prior season, or upcoming game state.
static func save_installed_earned_dynasty_workshop_layout(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_entry_app(app) or app.season!=null \
		or not app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("dynasty_closed",false): return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if source.is_empty() or not source.dynasty_third.has("close") \
		or source.dynasty_third.has("workshop_layout") or not source.dynasty_inherited_layout.is_empty(): return {}
	var prior: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.duplicate()
	var first: SeasonState=prior.dynasty
	var current: SeasonState=prior.dynasty_third
	var chair: Dictionary=source.dynasty_third.get("workshop_chair",source.dynasty_inherited_chair)
	var seed_value: int=int(first.career.start_basis.genesis.genesis.seed)
	var layout:=_earned_dynasty_workshop_layout(source.dynasty_third.close,seed_value,current,chair)
	if layout.is_empty(): return {}
	var value:=_earned_dynasty_compact_head(Owner._bytes_value(expected))
	if value.is_empty(): return {}
	var wire:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(value)
	if not Codec.same(wire,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	wire.workshop_layout=layout
	var staged:=_native_materialize(value)
	if staged.is_empty() or _earned_dynasty_compact_head(staged.value).is_empty(): return {}
	var unchanged:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(staged.value).duplicate()
	if not Codec.same(unchanged.workshop_layout,layout): return {}
	unchanged.erase("workshop_layout")
	if not Codec.same(unchanged,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages or again.pin!=source.pin \
		or not Codec.same(again.dynasty_compact_history,source.dynasty_compact_history): return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(staged.bytes,expected)
	saving=false
	return _bind_installed_earned_dynasty_third(staged.bytes,prior,current) if saved else {}


# Apply is free and next-season only. The exact paid ledger authorizes a
# single frozen receiver; no claim or arbitrary layout comes from the menu.
static func apply_installed_earned_dynasty_workshop_layout(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_entry_app(app) or app.season!=null \
		or not app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("dynasty_closed",false): return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if source.is_empty() or not source.dynasty_third.has("close") \
		or source.dynasty_third.has("workshop_applied") or not source.dynasty_inherited_application.is_empty(): return {}
	var prior: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.duplicate()
	var first: SeasonState=prior.dynasty
	var current: SeasonState=prior.dynasty_third
	var chair: Dictionary=source.dynasty_third.get("workshop_chair",source.dynasty_inherited_chair)
	var layout: Dictionary=source.dynasty_third.get("workshop_layout",source.dynasty_inherited_layout)
	var seed_value: int=int(first.career.start_basis.genesis.genesis.seed)
	var application:=_earned_dynasty_workshop_application(source.dynasty_third.close,seed_value,current,chair,layout)
	if application.is_empty(): return {}
	var value:=_earned_dynasty_compact_head(Owner._bytes_value(expected))
	if value.is_empty(): return {}
	var wire:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(value)
	if not Codec.same(wire,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	wire.workshop_applied=application
	var staged:=_native_materialize(value)
	if staged.is_empty() or _earned_dynasty_compact_head(staged.value).is_empty(): return {}
	var unchanged:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(staged.value).duplicate()
	if not Codec.same(unchanged.workshop_applied,application): return {}
	unchanged.erase("workshop_applied")
	if not Codec.same(unchanged,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages or again.pin!=source.pin \
		or not Codec.same(again.dynasty_compact_history,source.dynasty_compact_history): return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(staged.bytes,expected)
	saving=false
	return _bind_installed_earned_dynasty_third(staged.bytes,prior,current) if saved else {}


static func _earned_dynasty_second_source(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_entry_app(app) or app.season!=null \
			or not app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("dynasty_closed",false): return {}
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(app.saved_bytes)
	if source.is_empty() or source.dynasty_close.is_empty() or not source.dynasty_next.is_empty(): return {}
	return source


static func preview_installed_earned_dynasty_second(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	var source:=_earned_dynasty_second_source(app)
	if source.is_empty(): return {}
	var plan:=_earned_dynasty_prepare_second(source,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty)
	if plan.has("blocked"): return {"blocked":plan.blocked}
	if not plan.has("wire"): return {}
	return {"stat_loss":plan.stat_loss,"pitch_loss":plan.pitch_loss}


static func start_installed_earned_dynasty_second(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	var source:=_earned_dynasty_second_source(app)
	if source.is_empty(): return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var first: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty
	var plan:=_earned_dynasty_prepare_second(source,first)
	if not plan.has("wire"): return {}
	var value: Dictionary=Owner._bytes_value(expected)
	if not Codec.whole(value.get("version"),14,14): return {}
	value.version=15
	value.current.dynasty_next=plan.wire
	var body:=_native_encoded(value)
	if body.is_empty(): return {}
	var checked:=_installed_dugout_d1_parts(JSON.parse_string(body.get_string_from_utf8()))
	if checked.is_empty() or not Codec.same(checked.dynasty_next,plan.wire) \
			or not Codec.same(checked.dynasty_close,source.dynasty_close): return {}
	var again:=_earned_dynasty_second_source(app)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages: return {}
	var ordinary: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	_invalidate()
	_installed_dugout_d1_bound={"bytes":body,"page":source.pin,"draft":ordinary,"dynasty":first,
		"dynasty_next":plan.season}
	return installed_dugout_d1_view(body)

static func _earned_dynasty_third_source(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_entry_app(app) or app.season!=null \
			or app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("ordinal")!=2 \
			or not app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("dynasty_closed",false): return {}
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(app.saved_bytes)
	if source.is_empty() or not source.dynasty_next.has("close"): return {}
	return source

static func preview_installed_earned_dynasty_third(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_entry_app(app): return {}
	if int(app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("ordinal",0))>=3:
		var later:=_earned_dynasty_later_source(app)
		if later.is_empty(): return {}
		var plan:=_installed_earned_dynasty_later_plan(later)
		if plan.has("blocked"): return {"blocked":plan.blocked}
		return {"stat_loss":plan.stat_loss,"pitch_loss":plan.pitch_loss} if plan.has("wire") else {}
	var source:=_earned_dynasty_third_source(app)
	if source.is_empty(): return {}
	var plan:=_earned_dynasty_third_verified(source,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_next)
	if plan.has("blocked"): return {"blocked":plan.blocked}
	if not plan.has("wire"): return {}
	return {"stat_loss":plan.stat_loss,"pitch_loss":plan.pitch_loss}

static func start_installed_earned_dynasty_third(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_entry_app(app): return {}
	if int(app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("ordinal",0))>=3: return _start_installed_earned_dynasty_later(app)
	var source:=_earned_dynasty_third_source(app)
	if source.is_empty(): return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	if _earned_dynasty_third_prior(expected).is_empty(): return {}
	var prior: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.duplicate()
	var plan:=_earned_dynasty_third_verified(source,prior.dynasty,prior.dynasty_next)
	var staged:=_earned_dynasty_third_frame(expected,source,plan)
	if staged.is_empty(): return {}
	var again:=_earned_dynasty_third_source(app)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages or again.pin!=source.pin: return {}
	var body: PackedByteArray=staged.encoded
	# Preserve the exact v20 page before rotating the active head. If
	# interrupted, an exact inert archive may remain, not a dangling head.
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_keep_installed_dugout_d1_page(expected) and _write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	return _bind_installed_earned_dynasty_third(body,prior,staged.season)

static func _earned_dynasty_later_source(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_entry_app(app) or app.season!=null \
			or not app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("dynasty_closed",false): return {}
	var ordinal: Variant=app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("ordinal")
	if not Codec.whole(ordinal,3,ClubCareer.MAX_RUNS-1): return {}
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(app.saved_bytes,true)
	if source.is_empty() or source.dynasty_compact_ordinal!=ordinal or not source.dynasty_third.has("close"): return {}
	return source


static func _installed_earned_dynasty_later_plan(source: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var previous: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_third
	var applied: Dictionary=source.dynasty_third.get("workshop_applied",source.dynasty_inherited_application)
	return _earned_dynasty_carried_plan(_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty,previous,source.dynasty_third.close, \
		int(source.dynasty_compact_ordinal)+1,applied)


static func _start_installed_earned_dynasty_later(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	var source:=_earned_dynasty_later_source(app)
	if source.is_empty(): return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var terminal:=_earned_dynasty_compact_head(Owner._bytes_value(expected))
	if terminal.is_empty() or not Codec.same(_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(terminal),source.dynasty_third): return {}
	var prior: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.duplicate()
	var plan:=_installed_earned_dynasty_later_plan(source)
	var staged:=_earned_dynasty_later_birth_frame(expected,terminal,plan)
	if staged.is_empty() or _earned_dynasty_later_head(staged.staged).is_empty(): return {}
	var again:=_earned_dynasty_later_source(app)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages or again.pin!=source.pin \
			or not Codec.same(again.dynasty_compact_history,source.dynasty_compact_history): return {}
	var body: PackedByteArray=staged.encoded
	# An interrupted write can leave an inert content-addressed page, not
	# an active reference to a missing or partly written predecessor.
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_keep_installed_dugout_d1_page(expected) and _write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	return _bind_installed_earned_dynasty_third(body,prior,staged.season) if saved else {}


static func _bind_installed_earned_dynasty_third(body: PackedByteArray,prior: Dictionary,third: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	_invalidate()
	_installed_dugout_d1_bound={"bytes":body,"page":prior.page,"draft":prior.draft,
		"dynasty":prior.dynasty,"dynasty_next":prior.dynasty_next,"dynasty_third":third}
	return installed_dugout_d1_view(body)

static func _write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_opening(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,
		image: Dictionary,opening: Dictionary={}) -> Dictionary:
	if saving or source.dynasty_third.is_empty(): return {}
	var value:=_earned_dynasty_compact_head(Owner._bytes_value(expected))
	if value.is_empty(): return {}
	var active:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(value)
