	if active.has("close") or not Codec.same(active,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	var prior: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.duplicate()
	var plan:=_earned_dynasty_native_active_plan(source,prior.dynasty,prior.dynasty_next)
	active.season=image
	active.opening=opening
	var staged:=_native_materialize(value)
	if staged.is_empty() or _earned_dynasty_compact_head(staged.value).is_empty(): return {}
	var current:=_earned_dynasty_third_current(plan,_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(staged.value))
	if current==null: return {}
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages or not Codec.same(again.dynasty_third,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(staged.bytes,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	return _bind_installed_earned_dynasty_third(staged.bytes,prior,current)

# The payment producer has already reconstructed the exact pending wire and
# settled it independently of the live projection. For this single append we
# can compare the staged materialization without replaying the same history
# again in the writer. Cold admission always performs the complete replay.
static func _verified_carried_round2_append(source: Dictionary,after: Dictionary,paid: SeasonState) -> SeasonState:
	if paid==null or source.dynasty_compact_ordinal<4 or source.dynasty_compact_history.is_empty() \
			or paid.round_index!=2 or paid.phase!=SeasonState.Phase.REGULAR: return null
	var before: Dictionary=source.dynasty_third
	if not before.has("regular") or not before.regular.is_empty() or before.has("playoffs") \
			or before.has("close") or not after.has("regular") or after.regular.size()!=1: return null
	var witness: Dictionary=before.season.physical.pending
	var row: Variant=after.regular[0]
	if witness.is_empty() or not witness.human is Array or witness.human.size()!=13 \
			or witness.human[0]!=3 or not witness.reports is Array or witness.reports.size()!=2 \
			or not Codec.keys(row,["human","cpu","shop"]) or not Codec.same(row.human,witness.human) \
			or row.cpu!=2 or not row.shop is Array or not row.shop.is_empty(): return null
	for i: int in 2:
		var record: Variant=witness.reports[i]
		if not record is Dictionary or record.get("fixture")!=4+i: return null
	var unchanged: Dictionary=after.duplicate()
	unchanged.season=before.season; unchanged.regular=before.regular
	if not Codec.same(unchanged,before): return null
	var image: Dictionary=after.season
	if not image.physical.pending.is_empty() or before.season.physical.reports.size()!=2 \
			or not Codec.same(image.physical.reports,before.season.physical.reports+witness.reports): return null
	var captured:=Adapter.capture("dynasty",paid)
	return paid if not captured.is_empty() and Codec.same(captured,image) else null

static func _write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_regular(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,
		image: Dictionary, regular: Array, playoffs: Variant=null,
		verified_paid: SeasonState=null) -> Dictionary:
	if saving or source.dynasty_third.get("opening",{}).is_empty(): return {}
	var value:=_earned_dynasty_compact_head(Owner._bytes_value(expected))
	if value.is_empty(): return {}
	var active:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(value)
	if active.has("close") or not Codec.same(active,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	var prior: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.duplicate()
	active.season=image
	active.regular=regular
	if playoffs!=null:
		if not playoffs is Array or regular.size()!=9: return {}
		active.playoffs=playoffs
	elif source.dynasty_third.has("playoffs"): return {}
	var staged:=_native_materialize(value)
	if staged.is_empty() or _earned_dynasty_compact_head(staged.value).is_empty(): return {}
	var wire:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(staged.value)
	var current:=_verified_carried_round2_append(source,wire,verified_paid)
	if current==null:
		var plan:=_earned_dynasty_native_active_plan(source,prior.dynasty,prior.dynasty_next)
		current=_earned_dynasty_third_current(plan,wire)
	if current==null: return {}
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages or not Codec.same(again.dynasty_third,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(staged.bytes,expected)
	saving=false
	return _bind_installed_earned_dynasty_third(staged.bytes,prior,current) if saved else {}

# Bound warm champion only. Keep each previously verified page immutable;
# write and reread the new page before rotating the sole installed primary.
static func write_installed_dugout_d2_birth(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_shop_app(app) or app.season!=null: return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected)
	if source.is_empty() or not source.closed or source.tier not in [1,2]: return {}
	var tier: int=int(source.tier)+1
	if not app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("start_d%d_available"%tier,false): return {}
	var session: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	var earned: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d2.gd")
	var staged: Dictionary=earned.prepare_next(source.current,source.head,session) if tier==2 else earned.prepare_d3(source.current,source.head,session)
	if staged.is_empty(): return {}
	var value: Dictionary={"kind":INSTALLED_DUGOUT_D1_KIND,"version":3 if tier==2 else 5,"head":staged.head,"current":staged.current}
	var parts:=_installed_dugout_d1_parts(value)
	var native: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d2_local_draft(staged.current.ordinary) if tier==2 else _installed_dugout_d3_local_draft(staged.current.ordinary)
	if parts.is_empty() or native==null: return {}
	var body:=_native_encoded(value)
	if body.is_empty(): return {}
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages or again.pin!=source.pin: return {}
	saving=true
	var kept: bool=_keep_installed_dugout_d1_page(staged.page)
	for i: int in source.pages.size():
		var path:=_installed_dugout_d1_page_path(source.head.pages[i])
		if _base_archive_member(path).get("bytes",PackedByteArray())!=source.pages[i]:
			kept=false
			break
	var saved: bool=kept and _installed_dugout_d1_lane() and _matches(expected) and _write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	_invalidate()
	_installed_dugout_d1_bound={"bytes":body,"page":parts.pin,"draft":staged.season}
	return installed_dugout_d1_view(body)

# The installed D3 journal alone can issue the first Dynasty save. The
# prior 33 results, closed receipt, common account and pages are not rewritten.
static func write_installed_earned_dynasty_birth(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_shop_app(app) or app.season!=null \
			or not app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("d3_title_banked",false): return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected)
	if source.is_empty() or not source.closed or source.tier!=3 or not source.dynasty.is_empty(): return {}
	var ordinary: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	var earned: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d2.gd")
	var birth: Dictionary=earned.dynasty_draft_from_verified(source.current,source.head,ordinary,[])
	if birth.is_empty(): return {}
	return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_draft(source,expected,birth.season)

static func _write_installed_earned_dynasty_draft(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,season: SeasonState,opening: Dictionary={}) -> Dictionary:
	var draft:=Adapter.capture("dynasty",season)
	if draft.is_empty(): return {}
	var pending: bool=not season.physical.pending.is_empty()
	if pending and (source.dynasty.is_empty() or _earned_dynasty_opening_plan(season).is_empty()): return {}
	if not opening.is_empty() and (not source.dynasty_pending or pending or not _earned_dynasty_opening_settled(season,opening)): return {}
	var version: int=9 if not opening.is_empty() else (8 if pending else 7)
	var linked: Dictionary={"ordinary_origin":source.current,"dynasty":draft,"active":"dynasty"}
	if version==9: linked.round_one=opening.duplicate(true)
	var value: Dictionary={"kind":INSTALLED_DUGOUT_D1_KIND,"version":version,"head":source.head,"current":linked}
	if _installed_dugout_d1_parts(value).is_empty(): return {}
	var body:=_native_encoded(value)
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected)
	if body.is_empty() or again.is_empty() or again.pin!=source.pin or again.pages!=source.pages: return {}
	var ordinary: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	_invalidate()
	_installed_dugout_d1_bound={"bytes":body,"page":source.pin,"draft":ordinary,"dynasty":season}
	return installed_dugout_d1_view(body)

static func _pick_installed_earned_dynasty(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,id: String) -> Dictionary:
	var old: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty
	if old.phase!=SeasonState.Phase.DRAFT or old.picks.size()>=4 or not old.offers().has(id): return {}
	var draft: SeasonState=old.fork_live()
	if draft==null or not draft.choose_player(id) or draft.picks.size()!=old.picks.size()+1 \
			or draft.picks.back()!=id or not Codec.same(old.picks,draft.picks.slice(0,-1)): return {}
	# Keep cold replay and warm persisted snapshots in lockstep, including
	# the five opposing clubs and their authored profile on the fourth pick.
	var earned: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d2.gd")
	var ordinary: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	var rebuilt: Dictionary=earned.dynasty_draft_from_verified(source.current,source.head,ordinary,draft.picks)
	if rebuilt.is_empty() or not Codec.same(Adapter.capture("dynasty",draft),rebuilt.dynasty): return {}
	return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_draft(source,expected,draft)

static func pick_installed_dugout_d1(expected: PackedByteArray,id: String) -> Dictionary:
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected)
	if source.is_empty(): return {}
	if not source.dynasty.is_empty(): return _pick_installed_earned_dynasty(source,expected,id)
	var old: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	if source.closed or old.phase!=SeasonState.Phase.DRAFT or old.picks.size()>=4 or not old.offers().has(id): return {}
	var candidate: SeasonState=old.fork_live()
	if candidate==null or not candidate.choose_player(id) or candidate.picks.size()!=old.picks.size()+1 \
			or candidate.picks.back()!=id or not Codec.same(old.picks,candidate.picks.slice(0,-1)): return {}
	var recorded: Dictionary=Adapter.Ordinary.capture(candidate)
	if recorded.is_empty(): return {}
	var current: Dictionary=source.current.duplicate(true)
	current.ordinary=recorded
	var version: int=5 if source.tier==3 else (3 if source.tier==2 else 1)
	var value: Dictionary={"kind":INSTALLED_DUGOUT_D1_KIND,"version":version,"head":source.head,"current":current}
	var native: SeasonState
	if source.tier==3: native=_installed_dugout_d3_local_draft(recorded)
	elif source.tier==2: native=_installed_dugout_d2_local_draft(recorded)
	else: native=_installed_dugout_d1_local_draft(recorded)
	if _installed_dugout_d1_parts(value).is_empty() or native==null: return {}
	var body:=_native_encoded(value)
	var again:=_installed_dugout_d1_source()
	if body.is_empty() or again.is_empty() or again.bytes!=expected or again.pages!=source.pages: return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	_invalidate()
	_installed_dugout_d1_bound={"bytes":body,"page":source.pin,"draft":candidate}
	return installed_dugout_d1_view(body)

static func _bound_installed_equipment(expected: PackedByteArray,allow_held: bool=false) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_lane() or expected.is_empty() or expected!=_installed_equipment_bound: return {}
	var source:=_installed_dugout_d1_source()
	if source.is_empty() or not source.has("equipment") or source.bytes!=expected: return {}
	var draft:=EquipmentBase.local_draft(source.equipment,allow_held)
	if draft==null or not _matches(expected): return {}
	source.draft=draft
	return source

static func _bound_installed_named_game(expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	if expected.is_empty(): return {}
	if expected==_installed_equipment_bound: return _bound_installed_equipment(expected,true)
	if expected!=_installed_named_base_bound or not _installed_dugout_d1_lane(): return {}
	var source:=_installed_dugout_d1_source()
	if source.is_empty() or not source.has("named_base") or source.bytes!=expected: return {}
	var draft:=EarnedNamedBase.local_draft(source.named_base,true)
	if draft==null or not _matches(expected): return {}
	source.draft=draft
	return source

static func _installed_named_snapshot(source: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	return source.named_base if source.has("named_base") else source.equipment

static func _installed_named_witness(value: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var rounds: Array=value.get("rounds",[]) if value.kind==EarnedNamedBase.KIND else value.get("regular",[])
	return rounds.back().pending if not rounds.is_empty() else value.get("opening_paid",{})

static func _open_installed_equipment_match(app: Variant,expected: PackedByteArray) -> bool:
	var source:=_bound_installed_named_game(expected)
	if source.is_empty(): return false
	var bound: SeasonState=source.draft
	var slot:=EquipmentBase.play_slot(bound)
	if slot.is_empty() or not bound.physical.pending.is_empty(): return false
	var live: SeasonState=bound.fork_live()
	if live==null: return false
	var fixture: Dictionary=live.pending_fixture()
	if fixture.get("away")!=0 and fixture.get("home")!=0: return false
	if slot=="semifinal":
		if not Codec.same(EquipmentBase.DugoutTitle.semifinal_pair(live).get("club",{}),fixture): return false
	elif slot=="final":
		if fixture.get("id")!=32 or fixture.get("round")!=11 or fixture.get("neutral")!=true: return false
	elif fixture.get("id")!=3*bound.round_index or fixture.get("round")!=bound.round_index: return false
	var state: MatchState=Adapter.OrdinaryFixtures.match_for(live,fixture)
	var field: FieldDefinition=SeasonState.field_for_fixture(fixture,live)
	if state==null or field==null or not state.stadium.is_empty() or not state.sure_shot.enable_current_evidence(): return false
	app.season=live
	app._fixture_id=int(fixture.id)
	app._result_recorded=false
	app._result_saved=false
	app._open_match(state,int(fixture.home)==0,true,field.id)
	if not is_instance_valid(app.lab) or app.lab._match_state!=state or app.season!=live: return false
	_installed_dugout_d1_play={"app":weakref(app),"bytes":expected.duplicate(),"season":live,"state":state,
		"lab":weakref(app.lab),"field":field,"fixture":int(fixture.id),"named_game":true}
	return installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app)

static func _save_installed_equipment_managed(app: Variant,expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	if saving or _ticket!=null or not installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app): return {}
	var source:=_bound_installed_named_game(expected)
	if source.is_empty() or not source.draft.physical.pending.is_empty() \
			or not Codec.same(Adapter.capture("ordinary",app.season),_installed_named_snapshot(source).current): return {}
	var human: Array=Adapter.OrdinaryCapture._capture_bound(app,func(): return installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app))
	if human.size()!=13 or not installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app): return {}
	var candidate: SeasonState=source.draft.fork_live()
	if candidate==null: return {}
	candidate.physical.pending={"human":human.duplicate(true),"reports":[]}
	if EquipmentBase.pending_plan(candidate).is_empty(): return {}
	var current: Dictionary=Adapter.capture("ordinary",candidate)
	if current.is_empty() or not installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app): return {}
	# Leave the live lease in place until the original App closes the game.
	return _write_installed_equipment_opening(source,expected,current,{})

# Dedicated paired endpoint: no ordinary single-game lease or synthetic score.
static func _open_installed_doubleheader_match(app: Variant,expected: PackedByteArray) -> bool:
	var source:=_bound_installed_named_game(expected)
	if source.is_empty() or not source.has("named_base") or EarnedNamedBase.target(source.named_base)!="doubleheader": return false
	var paired: SeasonState=source.draft
	var live: SeasonState=paired.fork_live()
	if live==null: return false
	var opening: Dictionary=EarnedNamedBase.Paired.start(live)
	if opening.is_empty() or opening.fixture.get("id")!=0 or opening.fixture.get("round")!=0: return false
	var state: MatchState=opening.state
	var field: FieldDefinition=opening.field
	app.season=live
	app._fixture_id=int(opening.fixture.id)
	app._result_recorded=false
	app._result_saved=false
	app._open_match(state,int(opening.fixture.home)==0,true,field.id)
	if not is_instance_valid(app.lab) or app.lab._match_state!=state or app.season!=live: return false
	_installed_dugout_d1_play={"app":weakref(app),"bytes":expected.duplicate(),"season":live,"state":state,
		"lab":weakref(app.lab),"field":field,"fixture":int(opening.fixture.id),"pair_binding":opening.binding.duplicate(true)}
	return installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app)

static func _save_installed_doubleheader_managed(app: Variant,expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	if saving or _ticket!=null or not installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app): return {}
	var source:=_bound_installed_named_game(expected)
	if source.is_empty() or not source.has("named_base") or EarnedNamedBase.target(source.named_base)!="doubleheader" \
			or not source.draft.physical.pending.is_empty() \
			or not Codec.same(EarnedNamedBase.capture_current(source.named_base,app.season),source.named_base.current): return {}
	var human: Array=Adapter.OrdinaryCapture._capture_bound(app,func(): return installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app))
	if human.size()!=14 or not installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app): return {}
	var candidate: SeasonState=source.draft.fork_live()
	if candidate==null: return {}
	candidate.physical.pending={"human":human.duplicate(true),"reports":[],
		"binding":_installed_dugout_d1_play.pair_binding.duplicate(true)}
	return _write_installed_doubleheader_held(source,expected,candidate)

static func _write_installed_doubleheader_held(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,candidate: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	if saving or _ticket!=null or expected.is_empty() or expected!=_installed_named_base_bound or not _installed_dugout_d1_lane(): return {}
	var value: Dictionary=source.named_base.duplicate(true)
	value.current=EarnedNamedBase.capture_current(value,candidate)
	if value.current.is_empty(): return {}
	var materialized:=_native_materialize(value)
	if materialized.is_empty(): return {}
	var native:=EarnedNamedBase.local_draft(materialized.value,true)
	if native==null or native.physical.pending.is_empty(): return {}
	var again:=_installed_dugout_d1_source()
	if again.is_empty() or again.bytes!=expected or again.pages!=source.pages or not _matches(expected): return {}
	saving=true
	var saved:=_write_exact_bytes(materialized.bytes,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	_installed_named_base_bound=materialized.bytes
	return _installed_named_base_view(materialized.bytes,materialized.value,native)

# Only the exact installed App can open the authored opening fixture. The
# lease holds the native match and its one lab, not a caller-provided score.
static func open_installed_dugout_d1_match(app: Variant) -> bool:
	if not is_instance_valid(app) or app.get_script()!=load("res://src/presentation/season/successor_dynasty_owner_v1/app.gd") \
			or app.career_owner!=null or app.season!=null or is_instance_valid(app.lab): return false
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	if app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("state")=="doubleheader_draft":
		return _open_installed_doubleheader_match(app,expected)
	if app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("state") in ["equipment_draft","equipment_paid","earnings_draft","earnings_paid","scramble_draft","scramble_paid"]:
		return _open_installed_equipment_match(app,expected)
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if source.is_empty(): return false
	if not source.dynasty.is_empty(): return _open_installed_earned_dynasty(app,source,expected)
	var bound: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	if source.closed or not bound.physical.pending.is_empty(): return false
	if source.tier in [2,3] and not preload("earned_dugout_d2.gd").next_installed_available(bound): return false
	var earned: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd")
	var semifinal: bool=earned.final_regular_destination(bound)=="semifinal"
	var managed_final: bool=earned.settled_semifinal(bound)=="final_ready"
	if not semifinal and not managed_final and (not _installed_dugout_d1_history_shape(bound) or bound.round_index>=_installed_dugout_d1_regular_limit()):
		return false
	var live: SeasonState=bound.fork_live()
	if live==null: return false
	var fixture: Dictionary=live.pending_fixture()
	if semifinal:
		var pair: Dictionary=earned.semifinal_pair(live)
		if pair.is_empty() or not Codec.same(pair.club,fixture): return false
	elif managed_final:
		if fixture.get("id")!=32 or fixture.get("round")!=11 or fixture.get("neutral")!=true \
				or (fixture.get("away")!=0 and fixture.get("home")!=0): return false
	elif fixture.get("id",-1)!=live.round_index*3 or (fixture.get("away",-1)!=0 and fixture.get("home",-1)!=0): return false
	var state: MatchState=Adapter.OrdinaryFixtures.match_for(live,fixture)
	var field: FieldDefinition=SeasonState.field_for_fixture(fixture,live)
	if state==null or field==null or not state.stadium.is_empty() or not state.sure_shot.enable_current_evidence(): return false
	app.season=live
	app._fixture_id=int(fixture.id)
	app._result_recorded=false
	app._result_saved=false
	app._open_match(state,int(fixture.home)==0,true,field.id)
	if not is_instance_valid(app.lab) or app.lab._match_state!=state or app.season!=live: return false
	_installed_dugout_d1_play={"app":weakref(app),"bytes":expected.duplicate(),"season":live,"state":state,"lab":weakref(app.lab),"field":field,"fixture":int(fixture.id)}
	return installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app)

static func _installed_active_dynasty(source: Dictionary) -> SeasonState:
	var key: String="dynasty_third" if not source.dynasty_third.is_empty() else ("dynasty_next" if not source.dynasty_next.is_empty() else "dynasty")
	return _installed_dugout_d1_bound.get(key)

static func _installed_active_dynasty_image(source: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if not source.dynasty_third.is_empty(): return source.dynasty_third.season
	return source.dynasty_next.season if not source.dynasty_next.is_empty() else source.dynasty

static func _open_installed_earned_dynasty(app: Variant,source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray) -> bool:
	var carried: bool=not source.dynasty_next.is_empty()
	var third: bool=not source.dynasty_third.is_empty()
	var bound:=_installed_active_dynasty(source)
	if bound==null: return false
	if carried and not third and bound.round_index==0 and not _earned_dynasty_second_opener(bound): return false
	var later: bool=bound.round_index>0
	var postseason: bool=bound.phase in [SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL,SeasonState.Phase.FINAL]
	if carried and bound.build.pack_pending(): return false
	if bound.picks.size()!=4 or not bound.physical.pending.is_empty(): return false
	if later and not (_earned_dynasty_playoff_ready(bound) if postseason else _earned_dynasty_regular_ready(bound)): return false
	if not later and (bound.phase!=SeasonState.Phase.REGULAR or bound.round_index!=0 or not bound.results.is_empty() or not bound.physical.reports.is_empty()): return false
	var live: SeasonState=bound.fork_live()
	if live==null: return false
	var fixture: Dictionary=live.pending_fixture()
	if (not postseason and fixture.get("id")!=bound.round_index*3) or (fixture.get("away")!=0 and fixture.get("home")!=0): return false
	if not carried and bound.round_index==1 and (fixture.get("home")!=0 or fixture.get("away")!=4): return false
	var state: MatchState=Adapter.DynastyFixtures.match_for(live,fixture)
	var field: FieldDefinition=SeasonState.field_for_fixture(fixture,live)
	if state==null or field==null or not Codec.same(state.stadium,Owner.WorkshopApply.fixture(live,fixture)) \
		or not state.sure_shot.enable_current_evidence(): return false
	app.season=live
	app._fixture_id=int(fixture.id)
	app._result_recorded=false
	app._result_saved=false
	app._open_match(state,int(fixture.home)==0,true,field.id)
	if not is_instance_valid(app.lab) or app.lab._match_state!=state or app.season!=live: return false
	_installed_dugout_d1_play={"app":weakref(app),"bytes":expected.duplicate(),"season":live,"state":state,"lab":weakref(app.lab),
		"field":field,"fixture":int(fixture.id),"dynasty":true}
	return installed_earned_dynasty_match_bound(app)

static func installed_earned_dynasty_match_bound(app: Variant) -> bool:
	return _installed_dugout_d1_play.get("dynasty",false) and installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app)


static func installed_earned_dynasty_field_mounted(app: Variant) -> bool:
	if not installed_earned_dynasty_match_bound(app): return false
	var lab: PitchBatLab=app.lab
	var stadium: Dictionary=lab._match_state.stadium
	if stadium.is_empty(): return true
	if not lab._player_home or not Codec.same(lab.get_meta("stadium_snapshot",{}),stadium): return false
	var property: Variant=lab.get_meta("stadium_property",null)
	if not is_instance_valid(property) or not property is StadiumPropertyGeometry \
		or not property.objects.has(stadium.layout.receiver): return false
	var receiver: StadiumPitchReceiver=property.get_node_or_null("PitchReceiverPresentation")
	return receiver!=null and receiver.chair==property.objects[stadium.layout.receiver] \
		and lab._pitch_actor.receiver_router==receiver.route

static func _save_installed_earned_dynasty(app: Variant,source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	var carried: bool=not source.dynasty_next.is_empty()
	var original:=_installed_active_dynasty_image(source)
	if not installed_earned_dynasty_match_bound(app) or source.dynasty_pending or source.dynasty_home_pending \
			or not Codec.same(Adapter.capture("dynasty",app.season),original): return {}
	var previous:=_installed_active_dynasty(source)
	if previous==null or not previous.physical.pending.is_empty(): return {}
	var later: bool=previous.round_index>0
	var human: Array=Adapter.DynastyCapture.capture(app)
	if human.size()!=13 or not installed_earned_dynasty_match_bound(app): return {}
	var candidate: SeasonState=previous.fork_live()
	if candidate==null: return {}
	candidate.physical.pending=_earned_dynasty_pending_witness(candidate,human,[])
	var postseason: bool=candidate.phase in [SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL,SeasonState.Phase.FINAL]
	var plan:=_earned_dynasty_playoff_plan(candidate) if postseason else (_earned_dynasty_regular_plan(candidate) if later else _earned_dynasty_opening_plan(candidate))
	if plan.is_empty(): return {}
	var checked: Dictionary=Adapter.capture("dynasty",candidate)
	if checked.is_empty(): return {}
	var opening: Dictionary=checked.duplicate(true)
	opening.physical.pending={}
	if not Codec.same(opening,original) or not installed_earned_dynasty_match_bound(app): return {}
	if not source.dynasty_third.is_empty():
		if not later: return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_opening(source,expected,checked)
		var bracket: Variant=source.dynasty_third.get("playoffs",[]) if postseason else null
		return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_regular(source,expected,checked,source.dynasty_third.get("regular",[]),bracket)
	if carried:
		if postseason: return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_playoffs(source,expected,checked,source.dynasty_next.get("playoffs",[]))
		return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_regular(source,expected,checked,source.dynasty_next.get("regular",[])) if later \
			else _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_round(source,expected,checked,{})
	if later:
		var version: int=13 if postseason else (12 if source.dynasty_repeatable else 11)
		return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_progress(source,expected,candidate,checked,version,source.dynasty_shop,source.dynasty_regular,source.dynasty_playoffs)
	# Check the serialized numeric form while the match is still open.
	var materialized: Variant=JSON.parse_string(_native_encoded(checked).get_string_from_utf8())
	var proposed: Dictionary=source.duplicate()
	proposed.dynasty=materialized if materialized is Dictionary else {}
	proposed.dynasty_pending=true
	if proposed.dynasty.is_empty() or _installed_earned_dynasty_draft(proposed,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft)==null: return {}
	return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_draft(source,expected,candidate)

static func installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app: Variant) -> bool:
	var bind: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_play
	if bind.is_empty() or not is_instance_valid(app) or bind.app.get_ref()!=app or bind.bytes!=app.saved_bytes \
			or app.career_owner!=null or app.season!=bind.season or app._fixture_id!=bind.fixture \
			or not is_instance_valid(app.lab) or bind.lab.get_ref()!=app.lab: return false
	var lab: PitchBatLab=app.lab
	var season: SeasonState=bind.season
	return lab._match_state==bind.state and lab._field_definition==bind.field and lab._field_id==bind.field.id \
		and season.pending_fixture().get("id")==bind.fixture and lab._player_home==(int(season.pending_fixture().get("home",-1))==0) \
		and ClubCareer.same(lab._match_state.league_rules,season.league_rules) \
		and lab._match_state.pitch_catalog==season.build.pitch_catalog_signature() \
		and (Codec.same(lab._match_state.stadium,Owner.WorkshopApply.fixture(season,season.pending_fixture())) \
			if bind.get("dynasty",false) else lab._match_state.stadium.is_empty()) and season.physical.pending.is_empty()

static func save_installed_dugout_d1_managed(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app): return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	if _installed_dugout_d1_play.has("pair_binding"): return _save_installed_doubleheader_managed(app,expected)
	if _installed_dugout_d1_play.get("named_game",false): return _save_installed_equipment_managed(app,expected)
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if source.is_empty(): return {}
	if not source.dynasty.is_empty(): return _save_installed_earned_dynasty(app,source,expected)
	var previous: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	if not Codec.same(Adapter.Ordinary.capture(app.season),source.current.ordinary): return {}
	var human: Array=Adapter.OrdinaryCapture._capture_bound(app,func(): return installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app))
	if human.size()!=13 or not installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app): return {}
	var candidate: SeasonState=previous.fork_live()
	if candidate==null: return {}
	candidate.physical.pending={"human":human.duplicate(true),"reports":[]}
	var checked: Dictionary={}
	var managed_final: bool=candidate.phase==SeasonState.Phase.FINAL
	if managed_final: checked=_installed_dugout_d1_final_projection(candidate)
	elif candidate.phase==SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL: checked=_installed_dugout_d1_semifinal_projection(candidate)
	else: checked=BaseArchive.Core.MixedProof._project_mixed_round(candidate)
	if checked.is_empty(): return {}
	var projection: Dictionary=checked.projection
	if projection.has("error") or projection.has("fixture")!=(not managed_final) or not checked.get("source") is SeasonState: return {}
	var recorded: Dictionary=Adapter.Ordinary.capture(checked.source)
	var native:=_installed_dugout_d1_rebind(recorded,checked.source)
	if native==null or not native.physical.pending.reports.is_empty() \
			or not Codec.same(recorded.state.results,source.current.ordinary.state.results) \
			or not Codec.same(recorded.state.player_results,source.current.ordinary.state.player_results) \
			or not Codec.same(recorded.physical.reports,source.current.ordinary.physical.reports) \
			or not Codec.same(recorded.build,source.current.ordinary.build): return {}
	if not installed_dugout_d1_match_bound(app): return {}
	return _write_installed_dugout_d1_opening(source,expected,recorded,native)

# No command formats enter here. Each fixed producer has already proved the
# exact gameplay transition and independently materialized its canonical save.
static func _write_installed_dugout_d1_opening(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,recorded: Dictionary,native: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	if source.closed or native==null or not Codec.same(Adapter.Ordinary.capture(native),recorded): return {}
	if source.tier in [2,3]:
		var earned: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d2.gd")
		if earned.installed_fitted_tier(native)!=source.tier \
			or earned.installed_boundary(native) not in ["unpaid","paid","semifinal_unpaid","final_unpaid"]: return {}
	var current: Dictionary=source.current.duplicate(true)
	current.ordinary=recorded
	var value: Dictionary={"kind":INSTALLED_DUGOUT_D1_KIND,"version":5 if source.tier==3 else (3 if source.tier==2 else 1),"head":source.head,"current":current}
	if _installed_dugout_d1_parts(value).is_empty(): return {}
	var body:=_native_encoded(value)
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected)
	if body.is_empty() or again.is_empty() or again.bytes!=source.bytes or again.pages!=source.pages: return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	_invalidate()
	_installed_dugout_d1_bound={"bytes":body,"page":source.pin,"draft":native}
	return installed_dugout_d1_view(body)


static func _installed_dugout_d1_semifinal_projection(session: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	var checked: Dictionary=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd").project_semifinal(session)
	if checked.is_empty(): return {}
	return {"projection":checked.projection,"source":session,"session":session,"round":session.round_index,
		"count":checked.count,"managed_id":checked.fixture.id,"semifinal":true}

static func _installed_dugout_d1_final_projection(session: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	var result: Dictionary=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd").project_managed_final(session)
	return {} if result.is_empty() else {"projection":result,"source":session,"session":session,"round":session.round_index,
		"count":0,"managed_id":32,"managed_final":true}

static func _installed_dugout_d1_pending(expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	if not expected.is_empty() and expected in [_installed_equipment_bound,_installed_named_base_bound]:
		var source_equipment:=_bound_installed_named_game(expected)
		if source_equipment.is_empty(): return {}
		var draft: SeasonState=source_equipment.draft
		var job:=EquipmentBase.pending_plan(draft)
		if job.is_empty(): return {}
		job.merge({"source":source_equipment,"round":draft.round_index,
			"count":draft.physical.pending.reports.size(),"named_game":true})
		return job
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if source.is_empty(): return {}
	if not source.dynasty.is_empty():
		var carried: bool=not source.dynasty_next.is_empty()
		var dynasty:=_installed_active_dynasty(source)
		if dynasty==null: return {}
		var round: int=dynasty.round_index
		var post: bool=round==10
		var projection:=_earned_dynasty_playoff_plan(dynasty) if post else (_earned_dynasty_opening_plan(dynasty) if round==0 else _earned_dynasty_regular_plan(dynasty))
		if projection.is_empty(): return {}
		return {"source":source,"projection":projection,"session":dynasty,"count":dynasty.physical.pending.reports.size(),
			"round":round,"managed_id":int(dynasty.pending_fixture().id),"dynasty":true,"semifinal":post,"carried":carried}
	var plan:=_installed_dugout_d1_project_pending(_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft)
	if not plan.is_empty(): plan.source=source
	return plan

static func _installed_dugout_d1_project_pending(session: SeasonState) -> Dictionary:
	var earned: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d2.gd")
	if earned.installed_fitted_tier(session)>0 and earned.installed_boundary(session) not in ["unpaid","semifinal_unpaid","final_unpaid"]: return {}
	if session!=null and session.phase==SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL: return _installed_dugout_d1_semifinal_projection(session)
	if session!=null and session.phase==SeasonState.Phase.FINAL: return _installed_dugout_d1_final_projection(session)
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_history_shape(session) or session.round_index>=_installed_dugout_d1_regular_limit() \
			or session.physical.pending.is_empty(): return {}
	var checked: Dictionary=BaseArchive.Core.MixedProof._project_mixed_round(session)
	var result: Dictionary=checked.projection
	if result.has("error") or not result.get("candidate") is SeasonState or not checked.get("source") is SeasonState: return {}
	var count: int=session.physical.pending.reports.size()
	if not preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd").regular_projection_shape(session.round_index,count,result.has("fixture")): return {}
	return {"projection":result,"session":checked.source,"count":count,
		"round":session.round_index,"managed_id":session.round_index*3}

static func _installed_dugout_d1_round_app(app: Variant) -> bool:
	return is_instance_valid(app) and app.get_script()==load("res://src/presentation/season/successor_dynasty_owner_v1/app.gd") \
		and app.career_owner==null and app.season==null and not is_instance_valid(app.lab) \
		and app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("state") in ["unpaid","semifinal_unpaid","final_unpaid","dynasty_pending","equipment_held","earnings_held","scramble_held"]

# Only the actual OrdinaryRunner's completion for the projected fixture can
# append one receipt. No score/fixture selector or fallback save is exposed.
static func start_installed_dugout_d1_cpu(app: Variant) -> bool:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_round_app(app) or not _installed_dugout_d1_cpu.is_empty() or is_instance_valid(app.ai_runner): return false
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var round_plan:=_installed_dugout_d1_pending(expected)
	if round_plan.is_empty() or not round_plan.projection.get("fixture") is Dictionary: return false
	var fixture: Dictionary=round_plan.projection.fixture
	var plan: SeasonState=round_plan.projection.candidate
	if fixture.get("away")==0 or fixture.get("home")==0: return false
	if not round_plan.get("semifinal",false):
		var next_id: int=round_plan.managed_id+round_plan.count+1
		var next_round: int=int(round_plan.round) if next_id<30 else (10 if next_id<32 else 11)
		if fixture.get("id")!=next_id or fixture.get("round")!=next_round: return false
	var dynasty: bool=round_plan.get("dynasty",false)
	var fixtures: Script=Adapter.DynastyFixtures if dynasty else Adapter.OrdinaryFixtures
	var state: MatchState=fixtures.match_for(plan,fixture)
	var field: FieldDefinition=SeasonState.field_for_fixture(fixture,plan)
	if state==null or field==null: return false
	var worker: Script=load("res://src/gameplay/season/successor_dynasty_owner_v1/earned_dynasty_fast_npc.gd") if dynasty else null
	# The worker's scope admits only explicitly qualified journal stages.
	var accelerated: bool=worker!=null and worker.scoped(round_plan)
	var runner: Node=worker.new() if accelerated else (Adapter.DynastyRunner.new() if dynasty else Adapter.OrdinaryRunner.new())
	_installed_dugout_d1_cpu={"app":weakref(app),"runner":weakref(runner),"bytes":expected.duplicate(),"fixture":int(fixture.id),
		"count":round_plan.count,"dynasty":dynasty,"named_game":round_plan.get("named_game",false)}
	app.ai_runner=runner
	app.add_child(runner)
	runner.finished.connect(_finish_installed_dugout_d1_cpu.bind(app,runner),CONNECT_ONE_SHOT)
	runner.failed.connect(_fail_installed_dugout_d1_cpu.bind(app,runner),CONNECT_ONE_SHOT)
	var started: bool=runner.start(expected,round_plan) if accelerated else runner.start(state,fixtures.seed_for(plan,fixture),field.id)
	if started: return true
	_installed_dugout_d1_cpu={}
	app.ai_runner=null
	runner.queue_free()
	return false

static func _installed_dugout_d1_cpu_matches(app: Variant,runner: Node) -> bool:
	var lease: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_cpu
	return not lease.is_empty() and _installed_dugout_d1_round_app(app) and is_instance_valid(runner) \
		and lease.app.get_ref()==app and app.ai_runner==runner and lease.runner.get_ref()==runner \
		and lease.bytes==app.saved_bytes

static func cancel_installed_dugout_d1_cpu(app: Variant) -> bool:
	var runner: Node=app.ai_runner if is_instance_valid(app) else null
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_cpu_matches(app,runner): return false
	_installed_dugout_d1_cpu={}
	runner.cancel()
	app._installed_dugout_d1_cpu_done(runner,{},"CPU game canceled. Your saved round is unchanged.")
	return true

static func _fail_installed_dugout_d1_cpu(reason: String,app: Variant,runner: Node) -> void:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_cpu_matches(app,runner): return
	_installed_dugout_d1_cpu={}
	app._installed_dugout_d1_cpu_done(runner,{},reason)

static func _finish_installed_dugout_d1_cpu(report: Dictionary,app: Variant,runner: Node) -> void:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_cpu_matches(app,runner): return
	var lease: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_cpu
	_installed_dugout_d1_cpu={}
	var view:=_stage_installed_dugout_d1_cpu(lease,report)
	app._installed_dugout_d1_cpu_done(runner,view,"The CPU result could not be saved. Your previous checkpoint is unchanged." if view.is_empty() else "")

static func _stage_installed_dugout_d1_cpu(lease: Dictionary,report: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var round_plan:=_installed_dugout_d1_pending(lease.bytes)
	if round_plan.is_empty() or round_plan.count!=lease.count or not round_plan.projection.get("fixture") is Dictionary \
			or round_plan.projection.fixture.id!=lease.fixture or lease.get("dynasty",false)!=round_plan.get("dynasty",false): return {}
	if lease.get("named_game",false)!=round_plan.get("named_game",false): return {}
	if round_plan.get("named_game",false): return _stage_installed_equipment_cpu(round_plan,lease.bytes,report)
	if round_plan.get("dynasty",false): return _stage_installed_earned_dynasty_cpu(round_plan,lease.bytes,report)
	var admitted: Dictionary=Adapter.OrdinaryPending.stage_report(round_plan.session,report)
	if admitted.has("error") or not admitted.get("candidate") is SeasonState: return {}
	var candidate: SeasonState=admitted.candidate
	var before: SeasonState=round_plan.session
	var pending: Dictionary=candidate.physical.pending
	if pending.reports.size()!=int(lease.count)+1 or not Codec.same(pending.human,before.physical.pending.human) \
			or not Codec.same(pending.reports.slice(0,pending.reports.size()-1),before.physical.pending.reports): return {}
	var recorded: Dictionary=Adapter.Ordinary.capture(candidate)
	var native:=_installed_dugout_d1_rebind(recorded,candidate)
	var prior: Dictionary=round_plan.source.current.ordinary
	if native==null or native.physical.pending.reports.size()!=pending.reports.size() \
			or not Codec.same(recorded.state.results,prior.state.results) \
			or not Codec.same(recorded.state.player_results,prior.state.player_results) \
			or not Codec.same(recorded.physical.reports,prior.physical.reports) \
			or not Codec.same(recorded.build,prior.build): return {}
	return _write_installed_dugout_d1_opening(round_plan.source,lease.bytes,recorded,native)

# CPU reports enter only through the exact native runner lease. Payment
# is separate and replays the whole held opening with both saved reports.
static func _stage_installed_equipment_cpu(plan: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,report: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var admitted: Dictionary=Adapter.OrdinaryPending.stage_report(plan.session,report)
	var candidate: SeasonState=admitted.get("candidate")
	if candidate==null or admitted.has("error"): return {}
	var prior: Dictionary=_installed_named_snapshot(plan.source).current
	var current: Dictionary=Adapter.capture("ordinary",candidate)
	if current.is_empty(): return {}
	var pending: Dictionary=current.physical.pending
	var before: Dictionary=prior.physical.pending
	if pending.reports.size()!=before.reports.size()+1 or not Codec.same(pending.human,before.human) \
			or not Codec.same(pending.reports.slice(0,-1),before.reports): return {}
	var unchanged: Dictionary=current.duplicate(true)
	unchanged.physical.pending=before
	if not Codec.same(unchanged,prior): return {}
	return _write_installed_equipment_opening(plan.source,expected,current,{})

static func _write_installed_equipment_opening(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,current: Dictionary,
		witness: Dictionary,shop_log: Array=[],postseason: bool=false) -> Dictionary:
	var named: bool=source.has("named_base")
	var bound: PackedByteArray=_installed_named_base_bound if named else _installed_equipment_bound
	if saving or _ticket!=null or expected.is_empty() or expected!=bound or not _installed_dugout_d1_lane(): return {}
	var value: Dictionary=_installed_named_snapshot(source).duplicate(true)
	value.current=current
	var journal_key: String="rounds" if named else "regular"
	var journal: Array=value.get(journal_key,[])
	if not witness.is_empty():
		if postseason:
			var bracket: Array=value.get("playoffs",[])
			if journal.size()!=EquipmentBase.REGULAR_LIMIT-int(not named) or bracket.size()>=2 \
					or (not named and not value.has("opening_paid")): return {}
			bracket.append(witness.duplicate(true))
			value.playoffs=bracket
		elif named or value.has("opening_paid"):
			journal.append({"pending":witness.duplicate(true),"shop":[]})
		else: value.opening_paid=witness.duplicate(true)
	if not shop_log.is_empty():
		if journal.is_empty():
			if named: return {}
			value.opening_shop=shop_log.duplicate(true)
		else: journal.back().shop=shop_log.duplicate(true)
	if not journal.is_empty(): value[journal_key]=journal
	var materialized:=_native_materialize(value)
	if materialized.is_empty(): return {}
	var parsed: Dictionary=materialized.value
	var body: PackedByteArray=materialized.bytes
	var native:=EarnedNamedBase.local_draft(parsed,true) if named else EquipmentBase.local_draft(parsed,true)
	if native==null or not Codec.same(Adapter.capture("ordinary",native),current): return {}
	var again:=_installed_dugout_d1_source()
	if again.is_empty() or again.bytes!=expected or again.pages!=source.pages or not _matches(expected): return {}
	saving=true
	var saved:=_write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	if named:
		_installed_named_base_bound=body
		return _installed_named_base_view(body,parsed,native)
	_installed_equipment_bound=body
	return _installed_equipment_view(body,parsed,native)

static func _stage_installed_earned_dynasty_cpu(round_plan: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,report: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var prior: SeasonState=round_plan.session
	var source: Dictionary=round_plan.source
	var admitted: Dictionary=Adapter.DynastyPending.stage_report(prior,report)
	var draft: SeasonState=admitted.get("candidate")
	if draft==null or admitted.has("error"): return {}
	var after: Dictionary=Adapter.capture("dynasty",draft)
	if after.is_empty(): return {}
	var pending: Dictionary=after.physical.pending
	var carried: bool=round_plan.get("carried",false)
	var original:=_installed_active_dynasty_image(source)
	var before: Dictionary=original.physical.pending
	if pending.reports.size()!=before.reports.size()+1 or not Codec.same(pending.human,before.human) \
			or not Codec.same(pending.reports.slice(0,-1),before.reports): return {}
	var same_history: Dictionary=after.duplicate(true)
	same_history.physical.pending=before
	if not Codec.same(same_history,original): return {}
	if not source.dynasty_third.is_empty():
		if round_plan.round==0: return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_opening(source,expected,after)
		var bracket: Variant=source.dynasty_third.get("playoffs",[]) if round_plan.round==10 else null
		return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_regular(source,expected,after,source.dynasty_third.get("regular",[]),bracket)
	if carried:
		if round_plan.round==10:
			return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_playoffs(source,expected,after,source.dynasty_next.get("playoffs",[]))
		return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_regular(source,expected,after,source.dynasty_next.get("regular",[])) if round_plan.round>0 \
			else _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_round(source,expected,after,{})
	if round_plan.round>0:
		var version: int=13 if round_plan.round==10 else 12
		return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_progress(source,expected,draft,after,version,source.dynasty_shop,source.dynasty_regular,source.dynasty_playoffs)
	if _earned_dynasty_opening_plan(draft).is_empty(): return {}
	var proposed: Dictionary=source.duplicate()
	proposed.dynasty=JSON.parse_string(_native_encoded(after).get_string_from_utf8())
	if not proposed.dynasty is Dictionary or _installed_earned_dynasty_draft(proposed,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft)==null: return {}
	return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_draft(source,expected,draft)

static func _settle_installed_earned_dynasty(round_plan: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	if round_plan.round>0: return _settle_installed_earned_dynasty_regular(round_plan,expected)
	var source: Dictionary=round_plan.source
	var prior: SeasonState=round_plan.session
	var warm: SeasonState=round_plan.projection.candidate
	var carried: bool=round_plan.get("carried",false)
	var witness: Dictionary=_installed_active_dynasty_image(source).physical.pending
	if round_plan.count!=2 or not Codec.same(witness,prior.physical.pending) \
			or not _earned_dynasty_opening_settled(warm,witness): return {}
	# Re-execute the exact saved v8 payload with its serialized numeric types.
	# The settled writer and the cold verifier must derive the same receipt
	# journal. A warm report object is not the canonical payment input.
	var ordinary: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	var third: bool=not source.dynasty_third.is_empty()
	var materialized: SeasonState
	if third:
		var birth:=_earned_dynasty_native_active_plan(source,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_next)
		materialized=_earned_dynasty_third_current(birth,source.dynasty_third)
	else:
		materialized=_earned_dynasty_verified_second(source,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty) if carried \
			else _installed_earned_dynasty_draft(source,ordinary)
	if materialized==null: return {}
	var plan:=_earned_dynasty_opening_plan(materialized)
	if plan.is_empty() or plan.has("fixture"): return {}
	var next: SeasonState=plan.candidate
	if not Codec.same(next.results,warm.results) or not Codec.same(next.player_results,warm.player_results) \
			or next.cash()!=warm.cash() or not Codec.same(next.picks,warm.picks): return {}
	var after: Dictionary=Adapter.capture("dynasty",next)
	if after.is_empty(): return {}
	if carried:
		for id: int in range(6):
			var a: SeasonBuild=next.build if id==0 else next.opponents.clubs[str(id)].build
			var b: SeasonBuild=warm.build if id==0 else warm.opponents.clubs[str(id)].build
			if a.cash()!=b.cash() or not Codec.same(a._bank.view().rewards,b._bank.view().rewards): return {}
		if third: return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_opening(source,expected,after,witness)
		return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_round(source,expected,after,witness)
	var proposed: Dictionary=source.duplicate()
	proposed.dynasty=JSON.parse_string(_native_encoded(after).get_string_from_utf8())
	proposed.dynasty_pending=false
	proposed.dynasty_round_one=witness
	if not proposed.dynasty is Dictionary or _installed_earned_dynasty_draft(proposed,ordinary)==null: return {}
	return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_draft(source,expected,next,witness)

static func _settle_installed_earned_dynasty_regular(round_plan: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	var source: Dictionary=round_plan.source
	var warm: SeasonState=round_plan.projection.candidate
	var carried: bool=round_plan.get("carried",false)
	var third: bool=not source.dynasty_third.is_empty()
	var original:=_installed_active_dynasty_image(source)
	var witness: Dictionary=original.physical.pending
	if not Codec.same(witness,round_plan.session.physical.pending) or round_plan.count!=witness.reports.size(): return {}
	# Report keys use native number types. Payment comes only from the exact
	# disk proof; the transient producer and cold reader must give the same
	# results and six wallet deltas, not merely the same score.
	var materialized: SeasonState
	if third:
		var birth:=_earned_dynasty_native_active_plan(source,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_next)
		materialized=_earned_dynasty_third_current(birth,source.dynasty_third)
	else:
		materialized=_earned_dynasty_verified_second(source,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty) if carried \
			else _installed_earned_dynasty_draft(source,_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft)
	if materialized==null: return {}
	var post: bool=round_plan.round==10
	var proof:=_earned_dynasty_playoff_plan(materialized) if post else _earned_dynasty_regular_plan(materialized)
	if proof.is_empty() or proof.has("fixture"): return {}
	var next: SeasonState=proof.candidate
	if not Codec.same(next.results,warm.results) or not Codec.same(next.player_results,warm.player_results): return {}
	for id: int in range(6):
		var a: SeasonBuild=next.build if id==0 else next.opponents.clubs[str(id)].build
		var b: SeasonBuild=warm.build if id==0 else warm.opponents.clubs[str(id)].build
		if a.cash()!=b.cash() or not Codec.same(a._bank.view().rewards,b._bank.view().rewards): return {}
	var image:=Adapter.capture("dynasty",next)
	if image.is_empty(): return {}
	var row: Dictionary={"human":witness.human.duplicate(true),"cpu":round_plan.count,"shop":[]}
	if third:
		if post:
			var bracket: Array=source.dynasty_third.get("playoffs",[]).duplicate(true)
			bracket.append(row)
			return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_regular(source,expected,image,source.dynasty_third.regular,bracket)
		var regular: Array=source.dynasty_third.get("regular",[]).duplicate(true)
		regular.append(row)
		return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_regular(source,expected,image,regular,null,next)
	if carried:
		if post:
			var bracket: Array=source.dynasty_next.get("playoffs",[]).duplicate(true)
			bracket.append(row)
			return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_playoffs(source,expected,image,bracket)
		var regular: Array=source.dynasty_next.get("regular",[]).duplicate(true)
		regular.append(row)
		return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_regular(source,expected,image,regular)
	var journal: Array=source.dynasty_playoffs.duplicate(true) if post else source.dynasty_regular.duplicate(true)
	journal.append(row)
	return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_progress(source,expected,next,image,13 if post else 12,source.dynasty_shop,
		source.dynasty_regular if post else journal,journal if post else [])


static func settle_installed_dugout_d1_round(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_round_app(app) or is_instance_valid(app.ai_runner) or not _installed_dugout_d1_cpu.is_empty(): return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var round_plan:=_installed_dugout_d1_pending(expected)
	if round_plan.is_empty() or round_plan.projection.has("fixture"): return {}
	if round_plan.get("named_game",false):
		var witness: Dictionary=_installed_named_snapshot(round_plan.source).current.physical.pending
		var equipment_paid: SeasonState=round_plan.projection.candidate
		if not Codec.same(round_plan.session.physical.pending,witness): return {}
		var post: bool=round_plan.get("semifinal",false) or round_plan.get("managed_final",false)
		if post:
			if equipment_paid.round_index!=EquipmentBase.REGULAR_LIMIT or EquipmentBase.finish(equipment_paid).is_empty(): return {}
		elif not EquipmentBase._regular_boundary(equipment_paid,int(round_plan.round)+1): return {}
		var image: Dictionary=Adapter.capture("ordinary",equipment_paid)
		return {} if image.is_empty() else _write_installed_equipment_opening(round_plan.source,expected,image,witness,[],post)
	if round_plan.get("dynasty",false): return _settle_installed_earned_dynasty(round_plan,expected)
	var candidate: SeasonState=round_plan.projection.candidate
	var prior: Dictionary=round_plan.source.current.ordinary
	var count: int=round_plan.count
	if not candidate.physical.pending.is_empty(): return {}
	if round_plan.source.tier in [2,3] and preload("earned_dugout_d2.gd").installed_boundary(candidate)!="paid": return {}
	if round_plan.get("managed_final",false):
		if count!=0 or preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd").managed_final_destination(candidate).is_empty(): return {}
	elif round_plan.get("semifinal",false):
		var finish: String=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd").settled_semifinal(candidate)
		if finish.is_empty() or count!=(1 if finish=="final_ready" else 2): return {}
	else:
		if candidate.round_index!=int(round_plan.round)+1: return {}
		if candidate.round_index==_installed_dugout_d1_regular_limit():
			var finish: String=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd").final_regular_destination(candidate)
			if finish.is_empty() or count!=(2 if finish=="semifinal" else 5): return {}
		elif not _installed_dugout_d1_history_shape(candidate): return {}
	var recorded: Dictionary=Adapter.Ordinary.capture(candidate)
	var native:=_installed_dugout_d1_rebind(recorded,candidate)
	if native==null or native.round_index!=candidate.round_index or native.cash()!=candidate.cash() \
			or not Codec.same(recorded.state.results.slice(0,-(count+1)),prior.state.results) \
			or not Codec.same(recorded.state.player_results.slice(0,-1),prior.state.player_results): return {}
	if count==0:
		if not Codec.same(recorded.physical.reports,prior.physical.reports) or not prior.physical.pending.reports.is_empty(): return {}
	elif not Codec.same(recorded.physical.reports.slice(0,-count),prior.physical.reports) \
			or not Codec.same(recorded.physical.reports.slice(-count),prior.physical.pending.reports): return {}
	return _write_installed_dugout_d1_opening(round_plan.source,expected,recorded,native)

# Shop belongs to the current settled regular round. Access stays inside
# the same archived career and authored Build, never the generic Common owner.
static func _installed_dugout_d1_shop_source(expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	if not expected.is_empty() and expected in [_installed_equipment_bound,_installed_named_base_bound]:
		var equipment:=_bound_installed_named_game(expected)
		if equipment.is_empty(): return {}
		var witness:=_installed_named_witness(_installed_named_snapshot(equipment))
		var draft: SeasonState=equipment.draft
		return {"source":equipment,"session":draft,"named_game":true} \
			if not witness.is_empty() and EquipmentBase.shop_window(draft,witness) else {}
	var source:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if source.is_empty(): return {}
	if not source.dynasty_third.is_empty():
		var third: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_third
		return {"source":source,"session":third,"dynasty":true,"third":true} \
			if not source.dynasty_third.get("opening",{}).is_empty() and _installed_earned_dynasty_shop_window(third) else {}
	if not source.dynasty_next.is_empty():
		var carried: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty_next
		return {"source":source,"session":carried,"dynasty":true,"carried":true} \
			if not source.dynasty_next.get("opening",{}).is_empty() and _installed_earned_dynasty_shop_window(carried) else {}
	if not source.dynasty_round_one.is_empty():
		var dynasty: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.dynasty
		return {"source":source,"session":dynasty,"dynasty":true} if _installed_earned_dynasty_shop_window(dynasty) else {}
	var session: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	if source.closed or (source.tier in [2,3] and preload("earned_dugout_d2.gd").installed_boundary(session)!="paid"): return {}
	var earned: Script=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd")
	var final_ready: bool=earned.settled_semifinal(session)=="final_ready"
	if (not final_ready and not _installed_dugout_d1_history_shape(session) and earned.final_regular_destination(session)!="semifinal") \
			or session.round_index<1 or not session.physical.pending.is_empty() or not session.shop_available() \
			or session.build._visit.number!=session.round_index+int(final_ready): return {}
	return {"source":source,"session":session}

static func _installed_dugout_d1_entry_app(app: Variant) -> bool:
	return is_instance_valid(app) and app.get_script()==load("res://src/presentation/season/successor_dynasty_owner_v1/app.gd") \
		and app.career_owner==null and not is_instance_valid(app.lab) and not is_instance_valid(app.ai_runner) and not saving

static func _installed_dugout_d1_shop_app(app: Variant) -> bool:
	return _installed_dugout_d1_entry_app(app) \
		and app.installed_dugout_d1_draft.get("state") in ["paid","dynasty_paid","dynasty_postseason_ready","equipment_paid","earnings_paid","scramble_paid"]

static func _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(view: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if view.is_empty(): return {}
	var shop_source:=_installed_dugout_d1_shop_source(view.bytes)
	if shop_source.is_empty(): return {}
	var copy: SeasonState=shop_source.session.fork_live()
	return {"view":view,"session":copy} if copy!=null and copy.build._visit.open else {}

static func open_installed_dugout_d1_shop(app: Variant) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_shop_app(app) or app.season!=null: return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var shop_source:=_installed_dugout_d1_shop_source(expected)
	if shop_source.is_empty(): return {}
	var session: SeasonState=shop_source.session
	if session.build._visit.open: return _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(installed_dugout_d1_view(expected))
	var rev: int=session.build.revision()
	return _write_installed_dugout_d1_shop(shop_source,expected,{"id":"shop-open:%d"%rev,"rev":rev,"op":"open"})

static func write_installed_dugout_d1_shop(app: Variant,window: Variant,ticket: RefCounted) -> Dictionary:
	if not _installed_dugout_d1_shop_app(app) or app.season==null or not is_instance_valid(window) \
			or window.get_script()!=load("res://src/presentation/season/successor_dynasty_owner_v1/shop_window.gd") \
			or not app.installed_dugout_d1_shop_window(window) or not window.visible or not window._origin_matches(): return {}
	var expected: PackedByteArray=app.saved_bytes
	var shop_source:=_installed_dugout_d1_shop_source(expected)
	if shop_source.is_empty(): return {}
	if shop_source.get("dynasty",false):
		var active:=_installed_active_dynasty_image(shop_source.source)
		if not Codec.same(Adapter.capture("dynasty",app.season),active): return {}
	elif shop_source.get("named_game",false):
		if not Codec.same(Adapter.capture("ordinary",app.season),_installed_named_snapshot(shop_source.source).current): return {}
	elif not Codec.same(Adapter.Ordinary.capture(app.season),shop_source.source.current.ordinary): return {}
	var command: Dictionary=window.take_base_archive_shop(ticket)
	if command.is_empty() or command.get("op")=="open": return {}
	return _write_installed_dugout_d1_shop(shop_source,expected,command)

static func _write_installed_dugout_d1_shop(shop_source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,command: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if shop_source.get("named_game",false):
		var source: Dictionary=shop_source.source
		var snapshot:=_installed_named_snapshot(source)
		var previous: Dictionary=snapshot.current
		var rounds: Array=snapshot.get("rounds",[]) if source.has("named_base") else snapshot.get("regular",[])
		var journal: Array=(rounds.back().shop if not rounds.is_empty() else snapshot.get("opening_shop",[])).duplicate(true)
		if journal.size()>=128: return {}
		var witness:=_installed_named_witness(snapshot)
		var candidate:=EquipmentBase.shop_step(shop_source.session,witness,command)
		if candidate==null: return {}
		var current:=Adapter.capture("ordinary",candidate)
		if current.is_empty(): return {}
		for name: String in ["opponents","ownership","physical"]:
			if not Codec.same(current[name],previous[name]): return {}
		for name: String in ["results","player_results","picks","round_index","schedule","league_rules"]:
			if not Codec.same(current.state[name],previous.state[name]): return {}
		journal.append(command.duplicate(true))
		return _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(_write_installed_equipment_opening(source,expected,current,{},journal))
	if shop_source.get("dynasty",false): return _write_installed_earned_dynasty_shop(shop_source,expected,command)
	if not Codec.primitive(command) or not SeasonBuild.SHOP_OPS.has(str(command.get("op",""))): return {}
	var previous: SeasonState=shop_source.session
	var id: Variant=command.get("id")
	if not id is String or id.is_empty() or previous.build._requests.has(id) \
			or not Codec.whole(command.get("rev"),previous.build.revision(),previous.build.revision()): return {}
	var candidate: SeasonState=previous.fork_live()
	if candidate==null: return {}
	var next: SeasonBuild=candidate.build.candidate(command.duplicate(true))
	if next==null: return {}
	candidate.adopt_build(next)
	var before: Array=previous.build._events
	if candidate.build!=next or not candidate.build._visit.open or candidate.build._visit.number!=previous.build._visit.number \
			or next.revision()!=before.size()+1 or next._events.size()!=before.size()+1 \
			or not Codec.same(next._events.slice(0,before.size()),before) or not Codec.same(next._events.back(),command): return {}
	var image: Dictionary=Adapter.Ordinary.capture(candidate)
	if image.is_empty() or not Codec.same(image.physical,shop_source.source.current.ordinary.physical) \
			or not Codec.same(image.state.results,shop_source.source.current.ordinary.state.results) \
			or not Codec.same(image.state.player_results,shop_source.source.current.ordinary.state.player_results): return {}
	var native:=_installed_dugout_d1_rebind(image,candidate)
	if native==null or native.cash()!=next.cash(): return {}
	return _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(_write_installed_dugout_d1_opening(shop_source.source,expected,image,native))

static func _installed_earned_dynasty_shop_window(season: SeasonState) -> bool:
	return (_earned_dynasty_regular_ready(season) or _earned_dynasty_playoff_ready(season)) and season.physical.pending.is_empty() \
		and season.shop_available() and season.build._visit.number==season.round_index+int(season.phase==SeasonState.Phase.FINAL)

static func _installed_earned_dynasty_shop_step(previous: SeasonState,command: Dictionary) -> SeasonState:
	if not _installed_earned_dynasty_shop_window(previous) or not Codec.primitive(command) \
			or not SeasonBuild.SHOP_OPS.has(str(command.get("op",""))): return null
	var op: String=command.op
	var id: Variant=command.get("id")
	if not id is String or id.is_empty() or previous.build._requests.has(id) \
			or not Codec.whole(command.get("rev"),previous.build.revision(),previous.build.revision()) \
			or (previous.build._visit.open==(op=="open")): return null
	if op=="open" and (not Codec.keys(command,["id","rev","op"]) or id!="shop-open:%d"%previous.build.revision()): return null
	var candidate: SeasonState=previous.fork_live()
	if candidate==null: return null
	var next: SeasonBuild=candidate.build.candidate(command.duplicate(true))
	if next==null: return null
	candidate.adopt_build(next)
	var before: Array=previous.build._events
	if candidate.build!=next or not _installed_earned_dynasty_shop_window(candidate) or not next._visit.open \
			or next.revision()!=before.size()+1 or next._events.size()!=before.size()+1 \
			or not Codec.same(next._events.slice(0,-1),before) or not Codec.same(next._events.back(),command): return null
	return candidate

# Only the exact Shop ticket and live paid Season 3 visit can add a command.
# Reuse the proven append/reducer check. Cold will still replay every row.
static func _write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_shop(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,
		image: Dictionary,command: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if saving or source.dynasty_third.get("opening",{}).is_empty(): return {}
	var value:=_earned_dynasty_compact_head(Owner._bytes_value(expected))
	if value.is_empty(): return {}
	var active:=_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(value)
	if active.has("close") or not Codec.same(active,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	var prior: Dictionary=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.duplicate()
	if prior.dynasty_third.round_index>1:
		var rounds: Array=source.dynasty_third.get("regular",[]).duplicate(true)
		if rounds.size()!=prior.dynasty_third.round_index-1 or rounds.back().shop.size()>=128: return {}
		rounds.back().shop.append(command.duplicate(true))
		active.regular=rounds
	else:
		var journal: Array=source.dynasty_third.get("shop",[]).duplicate(true)
		if journal.size()>=128: return {}
		journal.append(command.duplicate(true))
		active.shop=journal
	active.season=image
	var staged:=_native_materialize(value)
	if staged.is_empty() or _earned_dynasty_compact_head(staged.value).is_empty(): return {}
	var next:=_verify_carried_shop_append(source.dynasty_third,_earned_dynasty_compact_wire(staged.value),prior.dynasty_third,command)
	if next==null: return {}
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected,true)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages or not Codec.same(again.dynasty_third,source.dynasty_third): return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(staged.bytes,expected)
	saving=false
	return _bind_installed_earned_dynasty_third(staged.bytes,prior,next) if saved else {}

static func _write_installed_earned_dynasty_shop(shop_source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,command: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var source: Dictionary=shop_source.source
	var carried: bool=shop_source.get("carried",false)
	var third: bool=shop_source.get("third",false)
	var prior:=_installed_active_dynasty_image(source)
	var first_shop: Array=source.dynasty_third.get("shop",[]) if third else \
		(source.dynasty_next.get("shop",[]) if carried else source.dynasty_shop)
	if shop_source.session.round_index==1 and first_shop.size()>=128: return {}
	var candidate:=_installed_earned_dynasty_shop_step(shop_source.session,command)
	if candidate==null: return {}
	var image:=Adapter.capture("dynasty",candidate)
	# A private player purchase cannot change an opponent, result, fixture,
	# field placement or the source of a prior reward.
	for field: String in ["state","opponents","physical","ownership"]:
		if image.is_empty() or not Codec.same(image.get(field),prior.get(field)): return {}
	if third:
		if candidate.phase==SeasonState.Phase.FINAL:
			var bracket: Array=source.dynasty_third.get("playoffs",[]).duplicate(true)
			if bracket.size()!=1 or bracket.back().shop.size()>=128: return {}
			bracket.back().shop.append(command.duplicate(true))
			return _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(_write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_regular(source,expected,image,source.dynasty_third.regular,bracket))
		return _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(_write_installed_earned_dynasty_third_shop(source,expected,image,command))
	if carried:
		if candidate.phase==SeasonState.Phase.FINAL:
			var playoffs: Array=source.dynasty_next.get("playoffs",[]).duplicate(true)
			if playoffs.size()!=1 or playoffs.back().shop.size()>=128: return {}
			playoffs.back().shop.append(command.duplicate(true))
			return _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(_write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_playoffs(source,expected,image,playoffs))
		if candidate.phase not in [SeasonState.Phase.REGULAR,SeasonState.Phase.SEMIFINAL]: return {}
		if candidate.round_index>1:
			var regular: Array=source.dynasty_next.get("regular",[]).duplicate(true)
			if regular.size()!=candidate.round_index-1 or regular.back().shop.size()>=128: return {}
			regular.back().shop.append(command.duplicate(true))
			return _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(_write_installed_earned_dynasty_second_regular(source,expected,image,regular,command))
		var value: Dictionary=Owner._bytes_value(expected)
		if not Codec.whole(value.get("version"),16,17): return {}
		var history: Array=first_shop.duplicate(true)
		history.append(command.duplicate(true))
		value.version=17
		value.current.dynasty_next.season=image
		value.current.dynasty_next.shop=history
		return _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(_commit_installed_earned_dynasty_second(source,expected,value,command))
	if candidate.phase==SeasonState.Phase.FINAL:
		var bracket: Array=source.dynasty_playoffs.duplicate(true)
		if bracket.size()!=1 or bracket.back().shop.size()>=128: return {}
		bracket.back().shop.append(command.duplicate(true))
		return _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(_write_installed_earned_dynasty_progress(source,expected,candidate,image,13,source.dynasty_shop,source.dynasty_regular,bracket))
	if candidate.round_index>1:
		var rounds: Array=source.dynasty_regular.duplicate(true)
		if rounds.size()!=candidate.round_index-1 or rounds.back().shop.size()>=128: return {}
		rounds.back().shop.append(command.duplicate(true))
		return _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(_write_installed_earned_dynasty_progress(source,expected,candidate,image,12,source.dynasty_shop,rounds))
	var history: Array=source.dynasty_shop.duplicate(true)
	history.append(command.duplicate(true))
	return _installed_dugout_d1_shop_view(_write_installed_earned_dynasty_progress(source,expected,candidate,image,10,history))

static func _write_installed_earned_dynasty_progress(source: Dictionary,expected: PackedByteArray,
		candidate: SeasonState,image: Dictionary,version: int,history: Array,regular: Array=[],playoffs: Array=[]) -> Dictionary:
	var value: Dictionary={"kind":INSTALLED_DUGOUT_D1_KIND,"version":version,"head":source.head,
		"current":{"ordinary_origin":source.current,"dynasty":image,"active":"dynasty","round_one":source.dynasty_round_one,"shop":history}}
	if version>=12: value.current.regular=regular
	if version==13: value.current.playoffs=playoffs
	if _installed_dugout_d1_parts(value).is_empty(): return {}
	var body:=_native_encoded(value)
	if body.is_empty(): return {}
	var proposed:=_installed_dugout_d1_parts(JSON.parse_string(body.get_string_from_utf8()))
	if proposed.is_empty() or not Codec.same(Adapter.capture("dynasty",candidate),proposed.dynasty): return {}
	var ordinary: SeasonState=_installed_dugout_d1_bound.draft
	var reconstructed:=_installed_earned_dynasty_draft(proposed,ordinary)
	if reconstructed==null: return {}
	var again:=_bound_installed_dugout_d1(expected)
	if again.is_empty() or again.pages!=source.pages: return {}
	saving=true
	var saved: bool=_write_exact_bytes(body,expected)
	saving=false
	if not saved: return {}
	_invalidate()
	_installed_dugout_d1_bound={"bytes":body,"page":source.pin,"draft":ordinary,"dynasty":reconstructed if version>=12 else candidate}
	return installed_dugout_d1_view(body)

static func stage_earned_dugout_d1(owner: RefCounted, expected: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	var offer:=earned_dugout_d1_offer(owner,expected)
	if offer.is_empty(): return {}
	var wrapped:=_base_archive_wrapper(expected)
	var staged:=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd").stage_birth(wrapped.current,wrapped.head,offer)
	return staged if not staged.is_empty() and base_archive_app_binding(owner,expected) else {}

# Qualification-side transaction only. Ordinary installed play remains closed
# until its own authenticated rollover and draft checkpoints are wired.
static func write_base_archive_earned_dugout_d1(owner: RefCounted, expected: PackedByteArray) -> bool:
	var staged:=stage_earned_dugout_d1(owner,expected)
	if staged.is_empty(): return false
	var pin: Dictionary=_base_archive_draft_pin
	if not base_archive_app_binding(owner,expected) or staged.head.pages.size()>BASE_ARCHIVE_QA_MAX_PAGES: return false
	var wire: PackedByteArray=BASE_ARCHIVE_HEADER.to_utf8_buffer()
	wire.append_array(BaseArchive._wire({"head":staged.head,"current":staged.current,
		"prior_primary_sha256":BaseArchive._hash(expected),"generation":BASE_ARCHIVE_EARNED_D1_GENERATION}))
	if wire.size()>MAX_BYTES: return false
	saving=true
	_base_archive_write_ticket=RefCounted.new()
	var saved: bool=_base_archive_install(_base_archive_write_ticket,pin.pair,staged.page,wire,true,pin.head,pin.pages)
	saving=false
	_invalidate()
	return saved

# Separate read-only validation. A literal predecessor is required for this
# first-generation witness; never silently fall back to another career.
static func read_base_archive_earned_dugout_d1() -> Dictionary:
	if saving or _ticket!=null or not _base_archive_qa() or not _base_archive_temps_clear(): return {}
	var pair:=_base_archive_pair()
	if pair.is_empty() or not pair.primary.present or not pair.backup.present: return {}
	var result:=_base_archive_wrapper(pair.primary.bytes)
	var source:=_base_archive_wrapper(pair.backup.bytes)
	if result.get("generation")!=BASE_ARCHIVE_EARNED_D1_GENERATION \
		or source.get("generation")!=BASE_ARCHIVE_FINAL_CLOSED_GENERATION \
		or result.prior_primary_sha256!=BaseArchive._hash(pair.backup.bytes): return {}
	var current_head:=BaseArchive._head(BaseArchive._wire(result.head))
	var prior_head:=BaseArchive._head(BaseArchive._wire(source.head))
	if current_head.is_empty() or prior_head.is_empty() or prior_head.pages.is_empty() \
		or current_head.pages.size()!=prior_head.pages.size()+1 \
		or current_head.pages.size()>BASE_ARCHIVE_QA_MAX_PAGES \
		or current_head.pages.slice(0,-1)!=prior_head.pages or not _base_archive_page_root(false): return {}
	var full: Array[PackedByteArray]=[]
	var prefix: Array[PackedByteArray]=[]
	for index: int in current_head.pages.size():
		var name: String=_base_archive_page_path(current_head.pages[index])
		var item:=_base_archive_member(name)
		if item.is_empty() or not item.present or BaseArchive._hash(item.bytes)!=current_head.pages[index] \
			or not _base_archive_absent(name+".tmp"): return {}
		full.append(item.bytes)
		if index<prior_head.pages.size(): prefix.append(item.bytes)
	if BaseArchive.append_head(BaseArchive._wire(source.head),full.back())!=BaseArchive._wire(result.head): return {}
	var verified: RefCounted=BaseArchive.restore_pages(BaseArchive._wire(source.head),BaseArchive._wire(source.current),prefix)
	if not _base_archive_final_closed(verified): return {}
	var contract=preload("earned_dugout_d1.gd")
	var offer: Dictionary=contract.from_closed_image(source.current,source.head)
	var staged: Dictionary=contract.stage_birth(source.current,source.head,offer)
	if staged.is_empty() or staged.page!=full.back() or not Codec.same(staged.current,result.current) \
		or not Codec.same(staged.head,result.head) or not Codec.same(result.current.common,verified.common): return {}
	var native: SeasonState=Adapter.Ordinary.restore_common(result.current.ordinary)
	if native==null or not Codec.same(Adapter.Ordinary.capture(native),result.current.ordinary) \
		or not _base_archive_temps_clear() or not Codec.same(_base_archive_pair(),pair) \
		or not _base_archive_pages_live(current_head,full): return {}
	return {"current":result.current,"head":current_head,"offer":offer}

# Exact process-verified writer readback, not a resample or external admission.
static func base_archive_app_result(previous: PackedByteArray) -> Dictionary:
	var proof: Dictionary=_base_archive_draft_pin
	if proof.is_empty() or not proof.pair.backup.present or proof.pair.backup.bytes!=previous \
			or not base_archive_app_binding(proof.owner,proof.pair.primary.bytes): return {}
	return {"owner":proof.owner,"bytes":proof.pair.primary.bytes.duplicate()}

static func _read_base_archive_live_source(allow_human: bool) -> RefCounted:
	if saving or _ticket!=null or not _base_archive_qa(): return null
	if _base_archive_draft_pin_live(_base_archive_draft_pin):
		var owner: RefCounted=_base_archive_draft_pin.owner
		return owner if allow_human or _base_archive_draft_image(owner,0) else null
	_base_archive_draft_pin={}
	var proof: Dictionary=_base_archive_load()
	if proof.is_empty() or not (_base_archive_draft_image(proof.owner,0) \
			or (allow_human and (_base_archive_report_count(proof.owner)>=0 or _base_archive_settled_image(proof.owner)
				or _base_archive_next_human_image(proof.owner) or _base_archive_regular_settled_image(proof.owner)
				or _base_archive_postseason_image(proof.owner) or _base_archive_semifinal_settled(proof.owner)
				or _base_archive_completed_closed(proof.owner) or _base_archive_completed_closed(proof.owner,true)
				or _base_archive_final_human(proof.owner) or _base_archive_final_settled(proof.owner)
				or _base_archive_final_closed(proof.owner)
